* **Open issues.** There is no CPU win: the fixed cost (~+1 us per view) is larger than the five stores per item it replaces even at 273 items per view; the per-flush dispatch + push-constant recording at the present (~1 us each, 3 - 8 per frame) could become ONE dispatch per frame if the jobs of all flushes were gathered into one table (the lists live in different ring blocks: a gather would copy them, which costs what it saves). The GPU is now the owner of the argument arena, which is what F5-2 needs: a cull pass that decides per record (and per cluster of a clustered record) what enters the arguments. D3D12 GBV's old `light.shadow trace` finding (above) is unchanged.

### 7.33 F5-1: cluster build for large static meshes (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-ad76e17abc1f20246`; Debug setting `native.clusters` (default off, run time), settings count 136)

Stage F5-1 of section 13. Files: `src/core/cluster_build.{h,cpp}` (pure: meshoptimizer clusters, the exact ordered-triple reorder, the content hash, the disk format; `tests/cluster_tests.cpp`, 6 cases), `src/gpuscene/cluster_table.{h,cpp}` (`ClusterStore`: the worker, the entries, the arena copies, the GPU cluster table), `src/d3d9/cluster_draw.cpp` (`Device::clusterApply`: eligibility, key, request / upload / packet), hooks in `d3d9/draw.cpp` (`drawIndexedPrimitive`, `fillCapture`, `replayBuild`), `d3d9/resources.cpp` (`Buffer` rename / destruction), `native/static_ops_records.cpp` (`recLiveWhy`), the setting (`core/settings.*`), `tools/d3d9test` case "clusters (F5-1)". Off = nothing exists: no thread, no table, no copies, no per-draw work beyond one bool test in `drawIndexedPrimitive`.

* **What a clustered range is.** For an indexed triangle-list draw of a static mesh (`Kind::StaticMesh`, not instanced, not blended, a rigid material vertex shader = `g_vInstanceData` at c32 and no skinning / SpeedTree / vegetation, POSITION FLOAT3 at offset 0 of stream 0, ring-copied buffers excluded) of **>= 1,024 triangles** (7.22's threshold) in a main z / solid or shadow depth pass, the store keeps per distinct range (key: vertex / index buffer id + the generation of their GPU copies, start index, primitives, base vertex, min index, num vertices, stream offset / stride, index size):
  1. the **cluster order** of its triangles: at the first draw copies of the range's indices (as local ids) and positions go to ONE worker thread (`meshopt_buildMeshlets`, 128 triangles / 128 vertices, cone weight 0.5; the cone itself is not kept: 7.22 measured it worthless); the result is `perm[k]` = the original triangle at position k plus a bounding sphere per cluster; the reordered list consists of exactly the engine's ORIGINAL ordered index triples: the builder's triangles are matched back to the originals by their sorted vertex triple, so a rotation by the builder cannot reach the output (the z pre-pass and the solid pass draw the same triangles with depth EQUAL: a triangle's depth depends on its vertex order). Cached on disk (`bin\rvk\cache\clusters\<content hash>.cl`: 24-byte header, the permutation, 24 bytes per cluster; the hash covers the builder version, the indices and the positions; a load re-verifies everything);
  2. a **COPY of the range's indices in cluster order in the mesh arena** (the engine's own arena copy stays as it is: other draws of the buffer, the RT scene's BLAS inputs, the vertex captures and the ocean floor read it; a reorder in place would also scramble any other draw whose range straddles it). The draw's packet is pointed at the copy (`indexBuffer` = the arena block's handle, `first` = the copy's offset / index size): the vertex streams, base vertex, everything else of the draw are untouched. A range's z, solid and cascade draws all find the same entry;
  3. the **cluster rows** in a GPU table `GpuTable<ClusterRow>` (32 B: sphere centre + radius, `firstIndex` = the cluster's first index in the arena buffer, `indexCount`, the entry id; 65,536 rows = 2 MiB, block allocator with a coalescing free list; the read-back check of the `GpuTable` recipe every 240th frame): nothing reads it yet (F5-2).
* **Lifetime and validity.** A range is Pending -> Built (worker) -> Ready (the copy + rows made on the render thread by the first draw that finds it Built; the engine's CPU index bytes of that moment must hash like the request's, else Failed). The entry dies with the engine index buffer's static copy: `Buffer::gpu()`'s rename and `~Buffer` / `orphan` queue the buffer id (`ClusterStore::bufferChanged`, any thread) and the render thread frees the arena copy (deferred, `freeMesh(.., true)`) and the rows (`drain`). A retained draw (packet cache) / op record (owned records) keeps the packet it was captured with: `CachedDraw::clusterWait` = the entry id (Pending at capture, or the entry whose copy the packet reads), `ClusterStore::stale(id, used)` makes `replayBuild` and `recLiveWhy` remake the draw / rebuild the record once the build has settled (the record then composes again and takes the copy: rebuild reason "resource load completed / clusters ready", 25 per 5 s window when the ranges become ready) or when the entry is gone (the index buffer was rewritten, the setting went off). Budgets: 192 MiB of copies, 65,536 rows, 256 pending builds (a refusal is counted and the range stays unclustered).
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 318 / 318 (6 new `clusters_*`: clusters tile the list with <= 128 triangles and a bounded size on a 7,200-triangle plane with and without rotated triangles; **every ordered index triple of the input is in the output exactly once** (sorted ordered triples equal, `tripleMultiset` equal) also with duplicate / degenerate / rotated copies; unusable input refused; `verify` catches a broken permutation / a hole / a sphere that does not hold its vertices / a list it does not cover; the content hash follows indices and positions, a rotated triangle is another mesh; the disk cache round-trips and refuses another hash, another mesh, a truncated or flipped file); settings count 136. `rvk_d3d9test --sync-validation` Vulkan and `--backend d3d12` (debug layer + GBV): PASS 0 failures, 0 validation errors, case "clusters (F5-1)": a 3,200-triangle grid drawn as a static mesh normally and with the setting on (first draw asks the worker, a later one takes the copy): **0 bytes differ**, both ranges A and B (the index buffer rewritten = a new generation = a new range, the old copy freed at the next drain), 2 ranges / 50 clusters / 6,400 triangles / 37.5 KiB, 10 draws on a copy, multiset checks 2 bad 0, **Vulkan GPU read-back of the arena copy** (TRANSFER_SRC was added to the arena's usage) 2 compared DIFFERING 0 (D3D12: not read back: the arena's resource state after a copy-source use is not ours to restore from here; the same `copyBuffer` path), switching the setting off leaves no arena bytes / rows and the picture equal. The in-store debug checks run in the game too: every upload re-checks the multiset digest of the engine's ordered triples against the written bytes (`multiset checks N bad 0`) and the first 24 uploads and every 8th afterwards are read back from the arena on Vulkan.
* **Game runs** (Full + the owned chain + persistent records, `-PlayerDefaults`, `native.clusters=true`, private copy, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f5/`): **first run Vulkan `-Validation` + GPU-assisted validation**, 3 spots, 180 s: PASS, 0 VUID, 0 error / warning lines; 132 ranges requested (the large static meshes of pub_interior, harbour_docks, jungle), 130 uploaded (2 Built, not drawn again), **5,274 -> 6,798 clusters, 584,510 triangles, 6.69 MiB of arena copies, table 6,798 of 65,536 rows**, builds 480 ms in total for all 132 (3.6 ms each, max 19.9 ms; the first sight of a range costs the engine thread the two CPU copies + a hash, a few tens of us), disk cache 132 files / 2.7 MB, multiset checks 130 bad 0, read-backs 6 DIFFERING 0, no failure of any kind, `native: owned records ... rebuilt (... resource load completed / clusters ready 25)`. Later runs: 91 - 132 of the 120 - 132 builds come from the disk cache (the 5 s lines: `builds 119 (113 from the disk cache, 20.5 ms of builds, max 9.3)`). D3D12 (debug layer without GBV): PASS, 0 errors, the same 130 ranges / 6,798 clusters. The rvk_rtwalk soak from the savegame (turning, cache in / out, `shadows.rt=near`, `lod 2x`, 175 s): PASS, 0 errors, 137 ranges seen (119 ready, 18 Built that no draw asked for again), 5.95 MiB, `owned persistent` / `owned static` MISS 0, DIFFERING 0. **Wrapper mode** (clusters work in every mode: the first version missed it because the draw's kind was only kept while a retained-draw capture was asked for, fixed in 8559dee): GPU-assisted validation run, pub_interior, 110 s: 0 VUID, 0 error lines, the same 130 ranges, read-backs 6 DIFFERING 0; the in-run A/B (`set native.clusters`, no owned chain): pub_interior toggle pairs 0.000 (bit-identical, same-state 0.000), harbour_docks 0.49 - 0.63 (max 67 - 81: the sea) against same-state 0.55; the Full GPU-assisted run repeated with the final code: PASS, 0 VUID, 0 errors, 131 ranges, compaction DIFFERING 0. RT scene / ocean floor / vertex captures read the engine's own buffers (the queries still hold the engine's index buffer), so they never see the copy: `shadows.rt=near` (the RT scene) and the player defaults' water (FFT ocean floor) were on in every run above.
* **Picture A/B** (the 7.11 method, `set native.clusters true|false` every 300 frames inside one hold with `shadows.rt=near`, a shot per 50 frames; `scratch/f5/abc_*`): pub_interior toggle pairs **0.000** (bit-identical, max 0 - 1 level), same-state 0.000; harbour_docks toggle pairs 0.04 - 0.08 (max 18 - 84: the sea) against same-state 0.067 (0.034 - 0.20); jungle toggle pairs 0.000 - 0.088 (max 1 - 2) against same-state 0.019 (max 0.25: wind). Inside the controls' range everywhere.
* **Cost** (`-Perf`, RisenTest.exe, vsync off, shared copy + lock, one run per spot with A / B / A / B states of 5,000+ frames; hold = every draw replays, so `clusterApply` is not on the path): engine + HAL CPU per frame **on 1.61 / off 1.66 ms** (pub_interior), **on 3.06 / off 3.12 ms** (harbour_docks): no cost (a 0.05 ms lower, drift). GPU (`-PerfFrame 5200`, 24 frames averaged): main submission **on 1.14 / off 1.11 ms**: no change (no cull yet: the same triangles). Memory: +6.2 - 6.7 MiB of arena copies per spot (the engine's copy of the same ranges stays: ~12 % of the ~50 MiB the mesh arena holds there), rows 2 MiB reserved.
* **Open issues / what F5-2 needs.** (1) A DrawRecord does not name its entry: `DrawRecord::args[3]` / `reserved0` can carry `rowFirst` / `rowCount` (an op record knows its draws' `CachedDraw::clusterWait` = the entry id; the entry gives the rows); (2) the cull must run per view from the record's windows (the view block's clip rows c0 .. c3, the object table's world rows: `f5::makeObjView` is the CPU reference) and write RUNS of visible clusters as argument records into the F2c-4c arena (an entry reserves `maxRuns` slots in its bucket's range: the compaction shader's scan then also compacts the runs; the frame's capacity / arguments per entry need a bound, the rest merges into the last run); (3) the persistent op records keep their packet until rebuilt: the 25 rebuilds per window happen at start-up and with every new range, a cluster-ready stamp per record is in (`recLiveWhy`); (4) Pending ranges keep the engine's packet: with the disk cache a range is Ready within 1 - 2 frames, a cold build up to a few frames (3.6 ms on the worker); (5) the arena read-back verification is Vulkan only; (6) the CPU-side first-sight cost has no timer (the walk shows no spike), add one when F5-2 turns on a per-frame cost; (7) GBV on D3D12 was only run through the d3d9test case (the game's GBV run is dominated by the old `light.shadow trace` finding, 7.32).

### 7.34 F5-2: GPU frustum cull of the owned draws of clustered meshes (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a7ad2a9f2bf74e42a` on main 7645bcc; Debug setting `native.cluster_cull` (default off, run time, needs `native.clusters` + `native.owned_persistent`), settings count 137)

Stage F5-2 of section 13. Files: `shaders/gpuscene/owned_compact.comp.hlsl` (the 7.32 pass extended), `src/gpuscene/owned_compact.{h,cpp}` (jobs of 32 bytes, capacities, the check frames' evaluation, the 5 s line), `src/core/cluster_cull.{h,cpp}` (the pure reference: planes, sphere test, run extraction; `tests/cluster_cull_tests.cpp`), `Device::ownedBuild` / `ownedFlush` / `cullCheckItem` / `cullCheckFinish` (`d3d9/owned_persist.cpp`), `ClusterStore::cullInfo / rowsAt` (`gpuscene/cluster_table.*`), `ObjectTable::worldRowsAt`, the setting (`core/settings.*`), `rvk_rhitest` "owned draws cluster cull (F5-2)", `rvk_d3d9test` "cluster cull (F5-2)". Off = nothing changes (the records carry no rows, the jobs no flag).

* **What it does.** A persistent record of a draw that reads the clustered copy (7.33) carries its cluster rows: `DrawRecord::args[3]` = `rowFirst | rowCount << 16`, `reserved0` = the entry id (set by `ownedBuild` only when the entry is Ready, its rows tile the copy contiguously (`ClusterStore::Entry::cullOk`, <= 4,096 rows), the packet is exactly the copy's range and VS window 2 is an object-table window; `ownedEnvHash` includes the setting, so the records are rewritten when it toggles). `ownedFlush` marks a flush **main z / solid** (view slots 0 / 1; the cascades' slots are never culled) and reserves **8 argument records (`kRunCap`) for every record that carries rows, 1 for the others** (per bucket capacity = the sum; the packet's maximum count is the capacity, the GPU's count decides). The compaction pass (jobs: first entry, entries, argument index, count index; capacity, flags) keeps its chunks of 64 entries; for a record with rows (kind 2) the group culls the entry cooperatively (entries one after the other, 64 threads over the rows): **object-to-clip rows** `r_i = c_i.x w0 + c_i.y w1 + c_i.z w2 + (0, 0, 0, c_i.w)` from the entry's view block (`c0..c3`, view slot of the list entry) and the object's world rows (`c33..c35` at window 2), planes left / right / bottom / top (`r3 +- r0 / r1`) and near (`r2`), **the far plane left open**, a sphere is outside when `d < -(r |n| + m (|w(c)| + r |r3.xyz|))` (m = 0.02 on the sides, 0.001 at the near plane: the camera jitter is applied after the matrices; the margin is a fraction of |w| over the sphere so it widens on BOTH sides of the camera plane), bits into a groupshared mask (4,096 clusters), then **runs of consecutive visible clusters** (`nextBit` over the mask, at most 8, the last one merged: from its start to the last visible cluster) become `{indexCount, 1, firstIndex, vertexOffset, firstInstance = the entry's position}` records, ordered with the other entries by the same inclusive scan; an entry with no visible cluster writes none. Guards (all in the shader): cull flag / tables non-null, `viewSlot < viewSlots`, `rowFirst + rowCount <= 65,536`, `rowCount <= 4,096`, window 2 non-null, **every row's range id equals the record's entry id (else the whole range, one record)**, runs clamped to the record's own `[firstIndex, firstIndex + indexCount)`, non-finite planes = the whole range, records beyond the bucket's capacity are dropped (the count is clamped: nothing is written outside the reservation).
* **Z and solid** cull with their own view slots but identical inputs (both slots carry the same camera, the object rows are the entity's one record): the same clusters come out, so the solid pass's depth EQUAL holds (and an entry culled in one pass only could not hurt: culling removes only what is outside the frustum). **Jitter**: the view block's matrices are unjittered, the 2 % margin is ~10 x the largest FSR jitter (half a render pixel); exercised by an A/B at `upscale.mode=performance` (1/2 render scale, jitter on): toggle pairs harbour_docks 0.516 (same-state 0.530), jungle 0.120 (0.125), inside the controls (the check frames' exact test has no jitter in it).
* **Two real bugs the check frames found** (both fixed, both would have made geometry vanish): (1) the **far plane** `(1 + m) r3 - r2` has a normal ~1e-3 of the others' (`1 + m - zf / (zf - zn)`), its distance loses ~10 world units in float: a cluster 17 units inside was culled; the far plane is left open now (the hardware clips beyond it anyway); (2) a **widening by `m * w`** tightens a plane for a sphere that straddles the camera plane (w < 0 at its centre: a 12 km terrain sphere behind the camera was culled): the margin is `m (|w(c)| + r |r3.xyz|)` now. The first unit test of the pure reference (`cluster_cull_matches_the_exact_frustum_test_and_only_widens_it`) now also runs spheres of terrain size (r x 300) and checks `culled with the margin => outside by the independent exact test`.
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 320 / 320 (2 new: reference vs `f5::makeObjView`/`sphereOutside` on 20,000 spheres incl. huge ones, run extraction covers every visible cluster with <= 8 runs and is exact below the cap; settings count 137). `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS Vulkan + D3D12 (debug layer + GBV), new case: the production shader on a synthetic cluster / view / object table (entries of 1, 5, 40, 200, 4,096 clusters, 4,097 (too many: whole), rows of another entry (whole), no object window (whole), 300 / 17 / 64 / 63 / 65; two views, a tight capacity (excess dropped, the guard words after the last bucket intact), a job without the flag, a view slot beyond the table): counts and **every record equal the CPU reference bit for bit** on both backends; `--sync-validation`: the new case clean (only the two old swapchain-acquire hints of the basic test remain). `rvk_d3d9test` (Vulkan, `--sync-validation`, `--backend d3d12`, with and without sync): PASS 0 failures, 0 validation errors; new case "cluster cull (F5-2)": a 3,200-triangle clustered grid drawn through the owned persistent path with the cull (object window from a test buffer, view slot 1 = the unit clip cube, x / y in [-3, 3] so most clusters lie outside): **pictures equal the normal draws (0 bytes) when (A) most clusters are culled, (B) the object is outside (no record: nothing drawn), (C) the frustum holds the whole grid (one whole-range run), (A2) half visible, (D) the setting off**; 4 items checked, 100 clusters (54 inside by the exact test, 55 drawn), 0 false negatives, 1 extra, indices 48,000 -> 30,720. `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded` 78 / 78 (the pass keeps one DXIL; push block 80 B).
* **Game runs** (Full, `-PlayerDefaults`, owned chain + persistent records + `native.clusters`, private copy `risen-test-vk-f5` instance 34, lock `rvk-f5`, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f5c/`; rvk.log `native: cluster cull (5 s)`): **first run Vulkan `-Validation` + GPU-assisted validation** (3 spots, 220 s, with the final shader: `val5`): PASS, 0 VUID, 0 error / warning lines, 10,942 items / 517,875 clusters checked, **0 false negatives**, 0 DIFFERING. D3D12 + debug layer (`RVK_NO_GBV=1`, `d3d12_nogbv2`, final code): 0 errors, 10,897 items / 516 k clusters checked exactly (0 false negatives, 0 inexact; 33 warnings, all #1477 "DSV format but the pipeline has no depth", old). D3D12 + GPU-based validation (pub_interior): 52 messages, **all from `light.shadow trace`** (the known old finding), none from `owned draws compact`. Soaks: the hero walking + turning from the savegame (`walk_fwd`, 150 s, `shadows.rt=near`, lod 2x, check every 3rd frame) 306,451 items / 11.5 M clusters checked, **0 false negatives**; the hero **spinning on the spot at 18 deg / s** (every view direction; `spin_on`, 120 s, check every 2nd frame) 221,875 items / 10.0 M clusters, 0 false negatives, with GPU-assisted validation (`spin_val`) 270,377 items / 12.7 M clusters, 0 false negatives, 0 VUID. Extras (clusters drawn that the exact test would cull) 1 - 4 % in the soaks, up to 13 % at the harbour (the 2 % margin + the runs merged over invisible clusters).
* **Picture A/B** (the 7.11 method, `set native.cluster_cull true|false` every 300 frames inside one hold with `shadows.rt=near`, a shot per 50 frames; `scratch/f5c/abcull_*`): pub_interior toggle pairs **0.0000** (7 of 7 bit-identical; same-state 0.0000), harbour_docks 0.678 (max 119: the sea) against same-state 0.682, jungle 0.009 (max 2) against same-state 0.019 (max 4).
* **Device loss, not the cull.** The scenario `rvk_rtwalk` with `burst=10 interval=1` (the back-buffer screenshot burst) while the hero spins gives `VK_ERROR_DEVICE_LOST` (GPU page fault, read at an invalid address) about 40 s into the run in **every** variant: cull on (twice), cull off with clusters on, and clusters off (`burst_base`); the same scenario without the burst never lost the device (cull on / off, GPU-assisted validation clean). The burst path needs its own look; this stage's bursts were therefore replaced by the check frames' exact test as the "no pop-in" evidence.
* **Triangles submitted** (the owned static draws of the main view, summed over a flush set, medians of the check frames in the holds; `rvk.log native: cluster cull`; these are the **owned** z / solid draws only: SpeedTrees / vegetation / skinned / alpha stay outside the owned path, so they are 25 - 55 % of the view's total, 7.22):

| scene | main z owned triangles before -> after | main solid before -> after | z + solid saved |
|---|---|---|---|
| pub_interior | 13.4 k -> 9.4 k (70 %) | 31.1 k -> 14.1 k (45 %) | 21 k |
| harbour_docks | 236 k -> 68 k (29 %) | 75 k -> 20 k (27 %) | 224 k |
| jungle | 533 k -> 411 k (77 %) | 68 k -> 44 k (65 %) | 145 k |
| savegame hold (lod 2x) | 290 k -> 156 k (54 %) | 40 k -> 13 k (32 %) | 161 k |

  The 1600x900 and 2560x1440 runs cull the same triangles (the cull does not depend on the resolution).
* **GPU time** (`-Perf` "GPU per frame" over 5 s windows of 2,400 - 3,000 frames, RisenTest.exe, vsync off, shared copy + lock, cull on / off / on / off inside ONE hold of each run; only windows wholly inside a state):

| scene | 1600x900 on / off | 2560x1440 on / off |
|---|---|---|
| pub_interior | 0.822 / 0.846 ms (-0.024) | 1.756 / 1.762 (-0.006) |
| harbour_docks | 2.153 / 2.203 (-0.050) | 4.355 / 4.449 (-0.094) |
| jungle | 4.780 / 4.786 (-0.006) | 6.278 / 6.270 (+0.008) |
| savegame hold | 3.294 / 3.295 (-0.001) | 3.842 / 3.861 (-0.019) |
| pub_interior, `vulkan.low_bandwidth=on` | 0.834 / 0.860 (-0.026) | |

  CPU engine+HAL: unchanged within 0.05 ms (pub 1.60 / 1.65, harbour 3.08 / 3.12, jungle 9.73 / 9.72, savegame 6.00 / 5.99 ms: the flush gets a capacity pass over its items, the compaction one more table read per record). `-PerfFrame` (24 frames averaged, 1600x900, `pf_*`): main submission pub_interior 1.124 on / 1.065 off (the solid pass 0.506 / 0.476: noise of the hold, the same pass also differs by 0.03 between the two off runs of 7.33), harbour_docks 2.265 / 2.350 (the two big 1600x900 passes 0.196 / 0.230 and 0.196 / 0.225), jungle 4.835 / 4.909 (z 0.289 / 0.298, solid 0.658 / 0.669).
* **Result: on the RX 7900 XTX the cull saves 0 - 0.09 ms (0 - 2 %) at 23 - 80 % fewer owned z / solid triangles** (7.22's estimate was 0.06 - 0.12 ms); `low_bandwidth=on` adds nothing (its images are not the geometry's). **680M extrapolation (a model, no 680M was available):** the saving is proportional to the triangles taken out and the cost of one triangle in the vertex / raster front end. From the harbour (224 k triangles saved = 0.05 - 0.09 ms at 1600x900 / 2560x1440 on the XTX) and the savegame / jungle (145 - 161 k = 0.00 - 0.02 ms) the XTX pays about 0.1 - 0.4 ns per culled triangle (most of them are cheap: clipped off-screen triangles cost a vertex fetch + the vertex shader, no raster). The 680M (12 CUs of RDNA2, one shader engine) has about 1/6 - 1/8 of the XTX's vertex-shading and primitive throughput, so **0.7 - 3 ns per triangle**: harbour 0.15 - 0.7 ms, jungle 0.1 - 0.45 ms, savegame 0.1 - 0.5 ms, pub 0.015 - 0.06 ms (a typical 0.2 - 0.4 ms, the lower end of 7.22's 0.3 - 0.7 ms: its estimate counted the cone-culled share too, which F5-2 does not do, 7.22 measured the cone worthless). The bandwidth / vertex fetch share on a shared-memory iGPU may add to it; this is unmeasured.
* **Cascades: what it would take** (not built). The shadow views' z ops are not persistent owned records (the owned persistent path serves the main view's z / solid ops only; the cascades go through `native/static_ops_shadow*`'s per-frame composition), so first the persistent records of the cascade ops (F2c-4a for the shadow views: record keys per cascade, the view blocks of the cascade slots 4.. already exist), then `flags` bit 1 (side planes only: the shader has it: a caster between the light and the cascade's near plane must stay, so near / far are open) and the cascade's own cull every frame it is rendered (`debug.shadow_stride` skips frames: the cull follows the render, not the present). The 0.4 - 0.6 M cascade triangles per rendered frame (7.22) are 20 - 25 % of the geometry of a frame, so it is worth about as much as the main views' cull again.
* **Open issues.** (1) The cull wins nothing measurable on the 7900 XTX; whether it pays on a 680M is an extrapolation (above): keep it Debug / default off until a 680M (or any vertex-bound iGPU) run exists. (2) `kRunCap` 8 + merge keeps 2 - 13 % extras; a finer split (runs per cluster tile) would trade capacity for fewer. (3) Exact check on D3D12 needed the object table's CPU rows by reference: fixed late (`ObjectTable::worldRowsAt` undid the offset shift only for Vulkan: D3D12 reported `exact evaluation impossible` for every item in the first D3D12 runs; the D3D12 triangle numbers matched Vulkan's). (4) The rvk_rtwalk burst device loss above (pre-existing; the burst path). (5) A culled-away draw still counts in the engine's bookkeeping (`draws`, texture touches): the CPU side stays identical, by design.

### 7.35 Device loss during a screenshot burst: the capture's mid-frame flush ran before the tables' flushes (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a7e6b8165ac7904ee`; no setting)

**Symptom** [C]: `rvk_rtwalk` with `burst=10 interval=1` (hero turning, Full, owned chain on) lost the device (`VK_ERROR_DEVICE_LOST`, `VK_EXT_device_fault`: GPU page fault, read at an invalid address, e.g. `0x2850fd000`) at the FIRST picture of the burst, ~40 s in (the log's first error is 0.5 s before `screenshot #1`); every variant of 7.34 (cull on / off, clusters on / off); the same walk without pictures never lost it.

**Cause** [C: code path; the fix removes the loss in 4 of 4 runs, the faulting build was reproduced by the F5-2 agent 4 times, not re-run to spare the desktop]: `HalDevice::SwapScreen` ran `captures.onPresent` (-> `Device::readBackBuffer` -> `readRenderTarget` -> `flushFrameAndWait`: submit + wait + `beginFrame`) BEFORE the gpuscene tables' `flush()` calls (illumination / lighting images / material / window / view / DrawRecord / cluster / object tables, the compaction jobs, billboards). Those flushes record into `uploadList()`, "copies that must land before the frame's draws": the upload command buffer runs first in the SAME submission as the main list. With the capture's mid-frame submission in between, the frame's draws were submitted without this frame's table uploads and the compaction (the indirect arguments, the DrawRecord / object rows the persistent owned draws index), which ran one submission LATER, behind the draws that read them: stale rows, stale or never written indirect arguments and counts, slots whose old content was already recycled = a read at an address that is no longer valid. A still camera (rvkcompare spots, `-Screenshots` of a frozen scene) rewrites nothing, so it hid the ordering error; a turning camera changes records every frame.

**Fix:** the capture block moved behind the tables' flush block (still before `dev_.present()` and the back buffer is not the swapchain image: the window code's recreate in between does not matter), so the mid-frame submission carries the frame's uploads. Rule: **anything that submits mid-frame (`flushFrameAndWait`) at present time must run after the present's flushes**; mid-frame flushes during the draws (`readImageLevel` diagnostics `RVK_NAN_BISECT` / `RVK_EXPOSURE_LOG`, frame generation's `flushFrameAndWait` before `BeforePresent`) have the same hazard for tables that are only flushed at the present: they are diagnostics / off-by-default paths, not changed.

**Evidence:** the faulting scenario (`rvk_rtwalk mode=walk turn=18 keys=TurnRight burst=10 interval=1 burst_at=20`, Full + `native.owned_persistent` chain + `shadows.rt=near`, lod 2x) with `-Validation` (0 validation errors, owned persistent 96.7 % reused) and 3 plain runs: no device loss, 10 of 10 pictures each. Full-game GPU-assisted validation was not usable (0.1 fps from the first loading frame: the run never reaches the walk).

**Follow-up (orchestrator, main):** the tables' flush block (with `scenetable::onPresent` / `f10::onPresent`) now runs FIRST in `SwapScreen`, right after `serviceSettings`, before the overlay, the `BeforePresent` injection (frame generation), the window / exclusive-fullscreen handling and the captures: every one of them may submit mid-frame (frame generation's context drop on a swapchain change calls `flushFrameAndWait`). The rule: **the present's table uploads are recorded before anything at the present that can submit.** Re-check on main: the burst scenario with `-Validation` (0 errors, 0 SYNC), plain, and plain + `native.clusters` + `native.cluster_cull` (FALSE NEGATIVES 0): PASS, 10 of 10 pictures, no device loss, persistent MISS 0. The `readImageLevel` diagnostics (`RVK_NAN_BISECT`, `RVK_EXPOSURE_LOG`) submit during the frame's draws, not at the present: still a hazard while they are on (developer knobs, off by default).

### 7.36 F3-1: owned shadow views, the sun cascades (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-ae86e3957cc784e53` on main 8b28224; Debug setting `native.owned_shadows`, default off, needs `native.owned_persistent`; settings count 138)

**What it is.** The 7.21 persistent owned path extended from the main view's z ops to the **sun cascades' static mesh z ops** (`ViewKind::Cascade`). Own setting rather than folded into `owned_persistent`: the cascades get scene forms (v1 + v2) only while it is on (`Device::setSceneShadows`: `sceneDecide` accepts `Shadow / ShadowDepth` static mesh draws), so A/B inside one hold is possible and the main view's path is bit-for-bit what it was with the setting off.
* **Records.** `recEnter` records the cascades' z mesh ops (group ShadowViews, list ZPass) with `Key.view = 1 + cascade` (main = 0); stats kept apart (`Store::shadowWindow`, `native: owned shadows records (5 s)`). Exclusions, stamps, audit, event-driven validity (7.30), loading watch, packet-cache retention: unchanged machinery (the same entity in three cascades = three records, three sets of slots). `OpRecord::lastSeen`: cascade records not looked at for 1,200 frames are dropped at the 5 s report (a cascade sees other ops as the camera moves; main records are not aged).
* **Owned frames.** `FrameTap::beginView` calls `StaticOps::ownedShadowViewBegin` for a cascade: the ops with a valid record leave the engine's z list (the ownedSelect / compactOwned of the main view, meshes only, the list's caps are not asked: ExecuteShadowMap runs the z list unconditionally); the `BeginZPass` detour calls `passBegin` for shadow views too; `ownedPass` draws them from persistent DrawRecords (view table slot `kViewSlotCascade0 + cascade`, the view from `composeView` of the shadow db's camera), one `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` per bucket via the F2c-4c compaction; side effects, late composition of refused ops, scrub: the main view's code. Per-view statistics (`ownedShadowW_`, `persistShadowW_`; line `native: owned shadows (5 s)`).
* **Changes the main path needed.** (1) The device's env epoch memo is per view slot (`ownedEnvEpoch_[8]`): with one memo per pass kind (z / solid) the order main z, cascade 0, 1, 2 moved the epoch at every pass and every persistent draw would have been derived again each frame (d3d9test checks it). (2) `ownedBulkBegin(solid, bulk = false)` for a cascade pass: no bulk list (its entered / left bookkeeping belongs to the main passes), the per-draw observers' loop as before. (3) `setOwnedFor` offers the object window to the cascades' static mesh ops; `sceneIssue` is on for them. (4) **Check frames are counted in cascade-rendered frames** (`shadowFrameSerial_`): `debug.shadow_stride` (default 2) renders the cascades on every 2nd frame while the camera stands still, and the frame number's check frames (multiples of 10) then never met a cascade view (first version: 0 check views, 90 warm-up per 5 s in the walks); `recDraw` verifies on `ownedShadowCheck_`.
* **Safety.** A MISS / DIFFERING in the cascades turns off only `owned_shadows` (`native: owned shadows turned off`), the cascades' records are dropped; the main view's owned path is untouched. Guards: slot < 8, view slot < table slots, the flush's existing CPU guards, `ownedBulk` not used.
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 320 / 0 (settings count 138 -> 139 with F3-2). `rvk_d3d9test` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors in all four combinations (Vulkan `--validation`, `+ --sync-validation`, `--backend d3d12` with and without sync); new check in "owned persistent": env epochs per view slot (stable while nothing changes, all move on a viewport change, `ownedEnvSame` per slot). `rvk_rhitest`: no RHI / shader change; its Vulkan `--sync-validation` run shows the same 2 old swapchain-acquire hints (frame slot 0 / 1) as before. Game runs in the private copy `risen-test-vk-f3` (instance 36, lock `rvk-f3`), Full + the nine-setting chain + `owned_shadows`, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f3/`: `-Validation` pub_interior (3 runs while building it), **three spots with `-Validation` + GPU-assisted validation: 0 errors, 0 VUID**; `rvk_rtwalk` from `Risen_Save_0005` (quick-load, walk + turn 12): walk 1 plain, walk 2 `shadows.enabled` + `shadows.rt=near`, walk 3 `shadows.enabled` + `debug.shadow_stride=1`: all PASS, 0 error lines, **cascade ops 96.8 - 100 % owned, composed late 0**, DIFFERING 0, MISS (scrub + check frames) 0 (walk 3: 624 k check-frame draws compared, 143 k scrubbed); the 7.35 scenario (`mode=walk turn=18 keys=TurnRight burst=10 interval=1`) with `-Validation` and plain: 10 of 10 pictures, no device loss, no validation error. Full-game GPU-assisted validation reaches the world only in the short rvkcompare spots (used above); the walks / bursts ran with the plain validation layer, not GPU-assisted (too slow to reach the walk).
* **Picture A/B** (the 7.11 method, `set native.owned_shadows true|false` every 300 frames inside one hold, shot every 50 frames, `freeze_wait=3200`): **pub_interior toggle pairs 0.0000 (7 of 7 bit-identical)**, harbour_docks 0.664 (same-state 0.702: the sea), jungle 0.0064 (same-state 0.0130; 4 bit-identical).
* **Numbers.** Cascade static mesh z ops per view: pub / harbour / jungle ~ 26 - 50, the savegame walk ~ 99 (x 3 cascades per frame), plus ~ 35 SpeedTree and ~ 5 actor ops per view that stay composed (not owned: F4 for cascades). CPU (savegame hold, lod 2x, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy + lock, rounds interleaved F / A0 / A1, A = the 7.26 arm S + owned_trees + owned_billboards + rt.table_entries, A1 = A0 + `owned_shadows`; engine + HAL ms per frame): **A0 8.00 / 7.96 / 7.51 = 7.82, A1 7.81 / 7.49 / 7.43 = 7.58: -0.24 ms (-0.33 over the first two rounds)**, Full defaults F 7.87 (so A1 is now 0.29 ms under F; A0 was 0.05 under); run-to-run sd 0.1 - 0.8 ms, so the saving is real but of the size of the noise per run; per cascade view the owned path costs ~ 20 - 50 us (compaction), at the harbour (26 draws per view) the saving is only -0.035 ms, jungle -0.10 ms (in-hold A/B, 20 - 38 windows per state: `h_owned_shadows_*`). GPU: unchanged (harbour -0.010 ms, jungle +0.015 ms on / off; savegame 3.51 vs 3.55). The estimate of the task (-0.3 .. -0.45 ms) is met only at the savegame; the cascades' mesh ops are ~ 40 % of the 0.57 ms shadow composition, the rest is trees and actors.
* **Terrain map / point-light atlas: not built.** The terrain map renders ~ 1 view per 5 s window (the sun step, 454 mesh ops in that view, `native: shadow views (5 s)`), the point-light atlas appeared in no test scene (only `rvk_pointshadow`): amortised they are < 1 % of the cascades' ops, and the atlas cells each have their own camera (a dynamic view slot per cell: the env epoch / records are per slot). The terrain map would fit the model with a fixed slot 7.
* **Open.** SpeedTree / skinned ops of the cascades (F4 for shadows); the cascades' records of a `shadows.map_size` change (viewport) are rewritten through the env epoch (not exercised in the game); toggling `owned_shadows` on at run time relies on the variant epoch bump (exercised in the A/B holds).

### 7.37 F3-2: cluster cull in the cascades (2026-11-07, same branch; Debug setting `native.cluster_cull_shadows`, default off, needs `native.cluster_cull` + `native.owned_shadows`; settings count 139)

The 7.34 compaction pass already had flag bit 1 (side planes only). `ownedFlush` now culls a cascade flush (view slots 4..7) when the setting is on: `cullFlags = 3`, so the four side planes of the cascade's view-projection (c0..c3 of its view block) decide, near and far stay open (a caster between the light and the near plane must stay; the far plane was already open); view kind 3 in the statistics (`[cascades: ...]` in `native: cluster cull (5 s)`). Ortho needs nothing special: row 3 is (0, 0, 0, 1), the planes are `1 +- x / y`, the margin is a fraction of |w| = 1 (2 % of the cascade's extent). Records of clustered draws carry their rows regardless of the view, so cascade draws without the flag draw whole ranges (d3d9test "I").
* **Gates.** d3d9test "cluster cull (F5-2)" (all four combinations PASS): cascade slot 4 with objects A (most clusters outside the sides), B (outside: nothing), A2 (half): 0 bytes differ from the normal draws; slot 5 whose NEAR plane lies beyond the object: **kept (pictures equal), while the same block on a main slot culls the whole draw** (the control); cascade flushes counted in view kind 3; setting off = whole range. Game: three spots with `-Validation` + GPU-assisted validation: 0 errors, FALSE NEGATIVES 0; the hero spinning at 18 deg / s with `shadows.enabled` + `shadows.rt=near`, compaction check frames every 2nd frame (`RVK_F2C4C_CHECK=2`): **29.9 k items / 1.21 M clusters checked, FALSE NEGATIVES 0**, extras 2.6 %; the burst scenario with `-Validation`: 10 of 10 pictures, 0 errors, FALSE NEGATIVES 0; picture A/B (`set native.cluster_cull_shadows` in one hold): pub_interior 0.0000 (7 of 7 bit-identical), harbour_docks 0.651 (same-state 0.685), jungle 0.0069 (0.0131).
* **Triangles** (the cascades' owned clustered z draws, per cascade flush, check-frame medians of the 5 s lines; 3 flushes per frame): spots 69.5 k -> 59.6 k (85.7 %), spots under GPU-assisted validation 63.7 k -> 45.8 k (71.9 %), the spin soak 114.1 k -> 79.0 k (69.3 %); the main z in the same runs 356 - 398 k -> 221 - 271 k.
* **GPU** (in-hold A/B, `native.cluster_cull_shadows` off / on / off / on, 21 - 67 windows of 5 s per state, RisenTest.exe, vsync off, shared copy + lock): harbour_docks **-0.012 ms** (2.169 / 2.157), jungle **0.000 ms** (4.830 / 4.830); CPU unchanged (+- 0.03). **The cascade cull saves nothing measurable on the RX 7900 XTX** (as the main view's 0 - 0.09 ms, 7.34): 14 - 31 % fewer cascade triangles of a pass that is not vertex bound there. The 680M extrapolation of 7.34 (0.7 - 3 ns per culled triangle) gives 0.04 - 0.3 ms per frame for the ~ 27 - 100 k triangles taken out of 3 cascades (a model, no iGPU run); keep it Debug / off until one exists.

### 7.38 What F3-3 (cached static shadow casters per sun step) would need

Today a cascade is redrawn completely every rendered frame (every 2nd while the camera stands still, `debug.shadow_stride`). F3-3 would keep the static casters' depth in a persistent per-cascade layer and redraw only the dynamic ones: (1) **an owned cascade target** (group 2048 already takes over the maps' target / viewport / clear: the static layer must be a second image per cascade plus a copy / "load from layer" at the start of the dynamic draw: a full-map read per rendered frame, so it only pays when the static draw costs more than that copy: 2048^2 depth ~ 16 MB x 3), (2) **invalidation inputs**: the cascade's view-projection incl. the engine's texel snapping (compare the matrix bit for bit: it changes whenever the camera crosses a texel step), the sun's 0.5 degree steps (`engine::shadowmaps::trueSunVector`), the cascade split fit, the map size / format, (3) **the static set's stamp**: the cascades' records already have per-op validity (stamps, event-driven dirty bits, 7.30): a set hash over (record, valid-at-entry, slot) + the ops entering / leaving the list (the list is rebuilt by the engine every frame: needs the 7.31 native collection or a compare of the sorted list), (4) **splitting static from dynamic ops**: static = the owned Mesh z records (this stage), dynamic = skinned, SpeedTree (swaying: unless frozen per step), particles; trees are 35 ops per view and would have to be owned or kept in the dynamic part, (5) consumers: the enhanced shadow mask, RT shadows (`shadows.rt` reads the cascades only for off-screen casters) and the GI read the final map: the layer copy must happen before them. Expected gain: the cascades' static part is ~ 2 of every 3 triangles; with a still camera the layer is valid for tens of frames; while walking it is invalid every frame (texel steps), so the gain is in the idle / dialogue / menu-adjacent cases, not in the walk.

### 7.39 F4-4: owned SpeedTrees in the sun cascades (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-ab72328856d53de29`, code commit f6e7f5f; no new setting: `native.owned_trees` + `native.owned_shadows`, count stays 139)

* **What.** The cascades' SpeedTree z ops (desc bit 0x10 << 16) are templated, planned and drawn exactly like the main view's (7.25 / 7.25b): `tro::OpKey` gained `view` (1 + cascade; a template holds that cascade's view slot), the cascades' tree view slots are table slots 8 + c (8..11; every v2 slot must stay < 16 and nothing used 8..11), `Device::treeEnvEpoch` memos per tree view slot, `sceneDecide` accepts SpeedTree draws in the shadow-depth phase, `ownedTreeObserversOk` also asks observers of shadow-phase trees. Frame tap: `treeViewBegin(owned, kind, cascade)` for every view, `compactOwned` takes tree ops out of a cascade's z list, `passBegin` calls `treePass(false)` for cascades. Statistics per view kind (`native: owned trees [main view|cascades] (5 s)`); a DIFFERING / MISS in the cascades turns off only the cascades' trees; `owned_shadows` off drops their templates. A/B knob: `RVK_F4_NO_SHADOW_TREES=1`.
* **Shadow rules the plan must follow** (found by the check frames: the first build had 51 DIFFERING and turned itself off): leaves cast from ONE leaf LOD, min(l0 + 1, LODs - 1), at alpha 1 (RenderLeafsZPass); fronds cast nothing when the leaf cards have more vertices than the fronds (no template, the op stays the engine's zero-draw op: ~21 % of the cascades' tree ops are "no record"). Tree billboards and vegetation cast no shadow ops (nothing to own). The terrain map (~1 view per 5 s) and point-light cells are not owned.
* **Per setting.** RT off / RT at the player defaults (no animated trees): main + cascade trees owned (the RT scene's observers are served from the owned runs: `observers shown` 255,840 in the walk). `shadows.rt=near` (wind): `rt_scene.animated_` makes `ownedTreesOk` false, so the main view AND the cascades keep composing trees (`0 owned` in every window of walk 2), as in 7.25b. `debug.shadow_stride` 1 / 2: check frames are counted in cascade-rendered frames (7.36).
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 320 / 0. `rvk_d3d9test` "owned trees (F4-3)" now also runs in the shadow-depth phase with view slot 9: PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan `--validation`, `+ --sync-validation`, D3D12 `--validation`, `+ --sync-validation` (the test resets PS c14 per draw: a second run saw the first run's leftover). `rvk_rhitest` (no RHI change): Vulkan `--validation` PASS, `--sync-validation` shows only the 2 old swapchain-acquire hints (frame slot 0 / 1), D3D12 PASS. Game, private copy `risen-test-vk-f4` (instance 37, lock rvk-f4), Full + the chain + owned_trees / billboards / shadows, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f4/`: three spots with `-Validation` + GPU-assisted validation (rt.allow=false): PASS, 0 errors, 0 VUID, cascades 360 of 450 views owned, DIFFERING 0, MISS 0, composed late 0; walks from `Risen_Save_0005` (quick-load, walk, turn): rt.allow=false stride 2, `shadows.rt=near`, stride 1 (897 views / 5 s), RT at defaults: all PASS, 0 `[error]`, cascades 80 % owned (0 owned with shadows.rt), DIFFERING 0, MISS 0 of ~600 - 1200 per window; turning burst with `-Validation`: PASS 10 pictures, 0 errors. The burst without validation ran last (queue1) and was not read separately.
* **Picture A/B** (`set <key> true|false` every 300 frames in one hold, rt.allow=false): `native.owned_shadows` pub_interior toggle 0.0594 (same-state 0.4129), jungle 0.1623 (0.0399), harbour_docks 0.7946 (0.7938: the sea); `native.owned_trees` pub_interior 0.0000 (bit-identical 6 of 6), jungle 0.0054 (0.0171), harbour_docks 1.028 (1.149). Toggle pairs are inside the same-state noise except jungle `owned_shadows` (0.16 vs 0.04, max level 5: the per-instance LOD start rule, an accepted Full picture change, 7.25b).
* **CPU** (savegame hold, RisenTest.exe, vsync off, lod 2x, shared copy + lock, 2 interleaved rounds, engine + HAL ms; RT at defaults): F 7.92, A1 (owned trees + owned_shadows, cascade trees composed via the env) 8.09, A2 (cascade trees owned) 8.01 = **A2 - A1 -0.09 ms, A2 - F +0.09** (run sd 0.2 - 1.0: neutral to slightly positive). In-hold toggle of `owned_shadows` at jungle (meshes + trees of the cascades): CPU +0.05 ms, GPU +0.22 ms (8 off / 18 on windows, the GPU shared with another agent's game: read as noise-limited, no win shown); the harbour_docks hold produced only on-windows (no off state), no number. GPU `-PerfFrame` was not run.
* **Composition CPU of the ops that stay the engine's** (new line `native: trees composition CPU`, savegame walk, ~150 frames per 5 s window, composed ops only): vegetation z 31 + solid 34 ms = **~0.43 ms / frame** (58 ops / frame, composed every frame), trees main z 17 + solid 29 ms (composed frames only, 20 %), cascades 9.6 ms, tree billboards 3.2 ms (0.02 ms / frame; owned in the main view by `owned_billboards`).
* **Vegetation (group 256) design, not built** (composition >= 0.1 ms / frame): per frame it reads each visible grid node's instances (44 bytes, SEH read), converts them (`grassInstance`) into one 1000-instance shared buffer, wraps it (1,500 - 1,700 wraps / 5 s) and uploads the bytes. Owning it = (1) a persistent GPU buffer per grid node and type holding the converted instance bytes, keyed by (grid node pointer, type, range first / count); (2) invalidation: the grid regenerates its arrays on cache-in / out and when instances change, so the node needs a generation (a hook on the grid's rebuild, or a cheap content stamp: pointer + count + a hash of the first / last cache line, plus a rolling full compare on check frames as the other owned stages do); (3) per op per frame only the node list (+0xa0 count, 0x118 entries) and the light set are read; draws become indirect draws over the node buffers (one record per node run, firstInstance = the node's offset), with the wind constant c12 (game time) in the view block; (4) the 1000-instance cursor / wrap bookkeeping (`leanVeg_`, admin +0x40) is still written for the engine's sake. Expected: most of the 0.43 ms goes, the cost moving to the node list read (~2 k nodes / frame) and buffer upload on change.
* **F4-5 (persistent per-tree records): skipped.** The plan's per-tree cost is two guarded reads; the owned view cost (7.25b) is dominated by the template lookups and rows, and the cascades add only ~0.1 ms net, so a record cache without an `UpdateLoD` post-hook (not reverse-engineered) saves little. Revisit with that hook.
* **Open.** GPU `-PerfFrame` of the cascades' trees; D3D12 game run of the cascade trees; the 680M test; vegetation as above.

### 7.40 F7-1: retained vegetation instance streams (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a168f4d6b1be1faef`, code commit 33ba587; Debug setting `native.owned_vegetation`, default off, Full only, needs the packet cache; settings count 140)

**Measurement first: the 7.39 design's premise did not hold.** New timers (`native: vegetation CPU (5 s)`, savegame hold, Full defaults, 5 s windows, ~400 frames each): of the vegetation's CPU per frame (composition 0.20 + `issue()` 0.22 = ~0.42 ms at that run's frame rate) the **node walk is 11 %, the instance read + conversion 7 %, the rest of the composition (`materialDraws` / `specialPassDraw` of the batches) ~20 % and `issue()` 42 %**. A vegetation op is small: 58 ops per frame (z + solid), **~48 visible grid nodes and ~43 instances per op, 1.04 draws per op** (1,126,400 nodes / 1,004,000 instances / 24,000 draws per 5 s over 23,200 ops). So the conversion + upload that per-node persistent buffers would remove is ~7 % (+ part of `issue()`), and indirect draws over node buffers (one command per node, ~48 per op) would multiply the draw count instead of shrinking it. **Per-instance fade / distance culling does not exist on the CPU in this engine**: the culling is per grid NODE (sphere + `CullCamera` in `RequestRenderOps`, 7.39 / D-contexts 4.1), the distance fade is the vertex shader's `o5.w = saturate(dist * c31.x + c31.y)` and the wind is c12 + the per-instance wind flag: nothing per instance to move to the GPU. The converted instance bytes are a pure function of the grid entry (0x2c bytes) and two type flags.

* **What was built (smaller than the 7.39 plan, exact by construction).** `StaticOps::vegRetain` (`static_ops_trees.cpp`): per op (context, type, list) the nodes' source entries are flattened (one guarded read per node, as before) and compared with the last build's flattened bytes (`memcmp`, plus the type's wind / lift flags): equal = the **persistent dynamic GPU buffer** of that op still holds the converted instances; different = convert and `LOCK_DISCARD`-rewrite it (grown to a power of two when needed). Every 10th frame a reused stream is also re-converted from the source and compared with the retained converted bytes (`DIFFERING`, a difference rebuilds and warns). The op's draws bind stream 1 = that buffer at `firstInstance * 0x2c` (frequency `0x80000001`) instead of the engine's shared 1000-instance ring + `vertexBytes`, so they carry no CPU bytes, the per-draw ring copy is gone and the draws are **packet-cache replayable** (`cacheable` needs no `vertexBytes` / `cpuStream`; they count in the trees' replayed share). The plan (`planGrass`: batches, light-set / flag cuts, cursor) and the engine's cursor bookkeeping (`leanVeg_`) are unchanged. Not used when `scene_ops_compare` is on (the engine's stream-1 bytes are compared there), in the cascades (no vegetation ops), without the packet cache, or for > 8 MiB per op (counted `fallback`). Streams of contexts not seen for 1,200 frames are dropped. Validity needs no engine hook: the compare is exact, so a rebuilt grid, a freed / reused node address or an in-place edit is simply a changed source. rvk.log `native: owned vegetation (5 s)`: ops, on a retained stream, reused / rebuilt (KiB), buffers grown, fallbacks, draws from retained streams, check frames, DIFFERING.
* **Deviations from the order.** The setting does not `needs` `native.owned_persistent` (nothing of it is used; it works in plain Full with the default packet cache). The per-node arena / indirect draws / GPU gather were not built: the measurement above says they would not pay (the cost is per op, not per instance), and the owned op records (7.14 / 7.17 recipe: stamps + read log + persistent DrawRecords) for a ~1-draw op is the step that would remove the composition + `issue()` (62 % of the vegetation's CPU); not started, see Open.
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 320 / 0 (settings 140). `rvk_d3d9test` new case "retained instance stream (F7-1)" (a dynamic buffer as stream 1, batches drawn from stream offsets 1 / 0, untouched across frames, then DISCARD-rewritten): PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan `--validation`, `+ --sync-validation`, D3D12 `--validation`, `+ --sync-validation`. `rvk_rhitest`: no RHI change, not re-run. No new GPU-address code (an ordinary dynamic vertex buffer through the existing HAL path, CPU guards: size cap, `lock` null check, 8 MiB limit). Game, private copy `risen-test-vk-f7` (instance 39, lock rvk-f7, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f7/`): **first run `-Validation` + `VK_KHRONOS_VALIDATION_VALIDATE_GPU_BASED=GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`** (jungle + harbour_docks, packet cache `verify_10`, rt.allow=false): PASS, 0 `[error]`, 0 VUID, no device loss, 135,046 vegetation ops all on retained streams (134,634 reused, 412 rebuilt), 13,470 check frames DIFFERING 0, packet cache verify 321,984 DIFFERING 0. Soaks (Full + the owned chain + owned trees / billboards / shadows + vegetation): spots jungle + harbour_docks + pub_interior with `verify_10` PASS (149,298 ops, 99.7 % reused, DIFFERING 0, packet verify 352,329 / 0); walk 1 quick-load rt.allow=false (587,372 ops, 99.6 % reused), walk 2 new-game beach (426,866 ops, 99.9 %), walk 3 quick-load with RT at the defaults (900,658 ops, 99.96 %): all PASS, 0 errors, DIFFERING 0, 0 fallbacks; the turning burst (`burst=10 interval=1 keys=TurnRight`) with `-Validation` and plain: PASS, 10 pictures, 0 errors, 0 VUID, **32 % of the ops rebuilt while turning** (276,886 of 859,844: the visible node set changes every few frames; a rebuild = the old conversion + upload plus the compare), DIFFERING 0. **Owned share of the vegetation ops: 100 % in every run** (no fallback, no refusal).
* **Picture A/B** (`set native.owned_vegetation true|false` every 300 frames inside one hold, a shot per 50 frames, rt.allow=false, game time frozen so wind is pinned): toggle pairs vs same-state pairs, mean absolute difference: jungle 2.94 vs 2.36 (max level 141 vs 248), swamp_camp 0.64 vs 1.33, lake_jungle 0.76 vs 2.67: the toggle is inside the same-state noise (water, particles, the engine's eye adaptation). The stronger evidence is exact: the check frames compare the retained converted bytes with a fresh conversion (0 differences in ~500,000 checks) and the stream is bound at the same instance offsets the composition computed. Wind motion in bursts: pictures not diffed against an off run (the wind is the unchanged c12 constant).
* **CPU** (savegame hold, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, Full defaults, shared copy + lock per run, other agents' games on the machine): round 1 off 7.725 ms (sd 0.85) / on 7.685 ms (sd 0.82); round 2 off 8.450 (sd 1.4) / on 11.049 (sd 3.8: a run disturbed by another game). In-hold toggles (jungle, swamp_camp) are also noise-dominated (sd 1.1 - 4.7 ms). **No CPU gain is resolvable**, as the measurement predicts (the change removes ~7 % conversion + the ring copy, and replaces `issue()` by the cheaper replay: an expected -0.05 .. -0.1 ms of the ~0.42 ms the vegetation costs); `-PerfFrame` GPU was not run (the draws read the same bytes from a device buffer instead of a ring copy: no GPU change expected; the perf windows' GPU 3.4 / 3.5 off / on).
* **Open.** (1) The remaining 62 % (composition of ~58 one-draw ops + `issue()` at ~0.25 ms) needs the owned op-record recipe (a persistent DrawRecord per op + the instance stream as a ref-addressed buffer, 7.21 / 7.32 compaction does not do instanceCount > 1), worth ~-0.2 ms; (2) the node walk (11 %, ~48 guarded entries per op) could be one bulk read of the 0x118-byte entries; (3) while turning a third of the ops rebuild: a per-node cache (keyed by grid node pointer + range, exact compare per node) would keep unchanged nodes' converted bytes but needs a gather to make batches contiguous; (4) CPU numbers need a quiet machine; (5) D3D12 game run not done.

### 7.41 F6-1: owned skinned actors (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a78c4294a246cac00` on main 271e5bb; Debug setting `native.owned_actors`, default off, needs `native.owned_persistent` + `native.owned_objects`; settings count 140)

Stage F6-1 of section 11: the actors' z / solid ops of the main view and z ops of the sun cascades get op records like the static meshes' (7.14 / 7.21 / 7.36) and are drawn from persistent DrawRecords. Files: `src/gpuscene/bone_table.{h,cpp}` (the bone table), `src/native/static_ops_actors_owned.cpp` (stamp, exclusions, image, serve, report), branches in `static_ops_records.cpp` (`recEnter` / `recEnd` / `recDraw`), `static_ops_owned.cpp` (`ownedSelect`, `ownedDrawOpPersistent`, `ownedPass`'s tag), `static_ops.cpp` (`setOwnedFor`, the packet cache and `sceneIssue` for the Actors group), `frame_tap.cpp` (`compactOwned` takes Animation ops), `d3d9/scene_draws.cpp` (`sceneDecide` accepts Skinned: `Device::setSceneActors`), `d3d9/owned_windows.h` (`Kind::Bones`, `OwnedStats::bones`), `static_ops_actors.cpp` (the composition publishes its bone state, the F6-0 measurement), the setting, `rvk_d3d9test` case "bone table (F6-1)".

**Design choice by measurement (F6-0, `RVK_F6_MEASURE=1`: `native: f6 measure (5 s) [main z | main solid | cascade z]`, no behaviour change; logs `rvk-work/scratch/f6/m1.log` / `m2.log`).**

| view of the frame | ops / frame (distinct actors) | vertices / frame | triangles | bones / op | morph ops (active weights) | composition per op / per frame |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| pub_interior, actors shown: main z | 8.5 - 8.9 (2.9) | 6.3 - 6.6 k | 9.1 - 9.6 k | 8.2 | 33 % (0.9 %) | 2.31 us / 21 us |
| main solid | 8.5 - 8.9 | 6.3 - 6.6 k | 9.1 - 9.6 k | 8.2 | 33 % | 2.90 us / 26 us |
| cascades (z) | 2.0 - 4.3 | 1.5 - 3.2 k | 2.2 - 4.6 k | 8.1 | 33 % | 3.39 us / 7 - 14 us |
| savegame hold: main z | 25.1 (7.7) | 18.9 k | 30.1 k | 11.7 | 40 % (0 %) | 2.49 us / 62 us |
| main solid | 25.1 | 18.9 k | 30.1 k | 11.7 | 40 % | 3.59 us / 90 us |
| cascades (z) | 20.6 (4.9; 14.5 distinct parts) | 15.7 k | 24.8 k | 12.1 | 40 % | 2.62 us / 54 us |

An actor part has ~ 750 vertices (mean 735 - 762, max 2,458 in a LOD 0 body, the LOD is always 0); a frame skins ~ 19 k vertices per view, a part is drawn 3.4 times a frame (z, solid, 1.4 cascades) = ~ 65 k skinned vertex-shader invocations per frame at the savegame. **(b) compute skinning once into a posed vertex buffer is rejected**: the vertex-shader skinning it would save is ~ 45 k vertices per frame (microseconds even on the 680M, ~ 0.03 - 0.06 ms by the 7.34 model), against a compute dispatch + barriers + 0.6 MB of posed-vertex writes / reads and a new static-draw front end for posed vertices (normals, tangents, uv, morph streams). The CPU prize is bounded too: the actors' composition is 54 us per frame at the pub, **100 - 207 us per frame at the savegame** (2.3 - 3.6 us per op: the retained packet cache and the lean path already made it cheap). **(a) per-part records + a per-frame bone image** is built.

**The bone table (`gpuscene::bonetable()`, `Kind::Bones`).** One persistent register image per (actor wrapper, primitive, fade) = VS window 2 (c31..c231, 201 registers = 3,216 bytes, a `GpuTable<BoneRecord>` of 1,024 records, the last one a zero tail pad): c31.xyz the three morph weights, c32.w the fade, `c33 + 3 i ..` the primitive's bone rows (the 3x4 transpose of palette entry `remap[start + i]`, exactly `writeBone`'s); c31.w / c32.xyz (the engine pool's carry-over, never read) are zero so every writer agrees. The key does not name the view: z, solid (fade 1) and every cascade of the frame read one slot, written once per frame by the first view that draws the part (`serve`: byte compare against the slot, rewrite only on a difference, **a second serve of the same frame with another content is refused = the GPU sees one value per frame**: `Conflict`, the draw keeps its upload). Idle slots (600 frames) are released and not handed out again for `kFramesInFlight + 1` presents; the table flushes first in the SwapScreen table block (7.35) into the frame's upload list; a GPU read-back every 240th frame compares the whole table (0 differing); line `native: bone table (5 s)`. Composed draws are served from it too (`setOwnedFor` -> `actorServeComposed`, from what the composition wrote into the register pool, **not** the draw's snapshot: a shader that does not read c31 / c32 has no live copy of them).

**Records.** `recExclusionActor` (overlay flags = the highlighted focus NPC, fade < 1, no light list / not table-lit for solid, shader class, blended solid shader, depth bias, > 66 bones, template property sets, an uncached resource: these stay composed; +0x2b8 "sort id" is non-zero for every skinned material shader and means nothing for the actors' passes: not an exclusion), `recStampActor` (wrapper, entity, illumination, variant, fade, view epoch, and one geometry hash over the HRA, its primitive, the primitive's bone remap, the visual's record, the LOD's vertex count, the material / shader fields and **the morph pick**: the three strongest targets decide the stream offsets of streams 1..3, which are part of the retained draw; the weights themselves only go into the bone image), the engine reads of the stamp memoised per actor instance and frame (morph pick). No event dependencies (`depRegister` / `depFast` are skipped: the full stamp is read every frame, ~ 1 us per op); `EVENT MISS` does not apply. The packet cache retains the Actors group's draws while the setting is on, `sceneIssue` is on for them, `Device::sceneDecide` gives skinned main-view (z / solid) and cascade draws their v1 + v2 scene forms, `r.vs1Offered` = every draw's window 2 was served from the bone table (else no record). Check frames compare as before (`recDraw`: derived vs composed scene draw, windows, DrawBlock) plus **`actorVerify`: the table image the composed draw wrote equals one built from the engine's palette now** (`check frames: N images compared, DIFFERING`); a composed draw that went with a capture variant (an observer asked for the vertex outputs of this very draw) is no scene draw by design and is not compared (`captureSkipped`). A DIFFERING turns `native.owned_actors` off (`native: owned actors turned off`), the actors' records are dropped, nothing else changes.

**Owned frames.** `FrameTap::compactOwned` takes Animation ops with a valid record out of the lists; `ownedDrawOpPersistent` serves the actor's bone window (`ownedActorWindow`: the second view of the frame finds the key served; else the engine slot's side effects the lean commit replays (`UpdateRenderMatrices` once per frame and actor, the touches of the HRA's resource and the material) and the image from the engine's palette), builds the persistent draws as for meshes (window 2 = the bone table's base, in the draw's signature), the observers see the draw through `ownedObserve` with the window's CPU twin loaded into the register file (RT skinning reads c31 / c33.. from it). A refused draw (`Conflict`, an observer's capture request, a stale record) is composed late at its place.

**Owned share of actor ops** (`native: owned actors (5 s)`): **RT scene on (player defaults): main z 100 %, cascades 100 %, main solid 0 %** (the RT scene asks a vertex-shader capture variant of every solid-pass character draw, `captureDraw`: such draws are neither retained nor scene draws); **`rt.allow=false`: main z 100 %, main solid 100 %, cascades 100 %**. Not owned in either: ops with an overlay flag (the focus NPC), fade < 1, a morph pick that changed this frame (rebuilt by the composition: 2 per blink), a part whose bone image conflicts. With upscaling on (`upscale.mode`) the whole owned path is gated off (as for the meshes), the composed actor draws still read the bone table.

* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 320 / 0 (settings count 139 -> 140). `rvk_d3d9test` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors in all four combinations (Vulkan `--validation`, `+ --sync-validation`, `--backend d3d12` with and without sync); new case "bone table (F6-1)" through the device's own path (`Device::splitStage` with a `Kind::Bones` window, `draws` of a translated vertex shader that skins its quad with c33..c35, adds c31 and scales by c32.w): the draw with junk registers and the served image equals the draw from the register file, the same content again writes nothing (`unchanged`, no slot uploaded), another content in the same frame is `Conflict` and leaves `out` alone (the draw reads the frame's first image), a new frame takes the new content (= the reference of the new registers), `servedNow`, guards (no wrapper / no registers / 0, 1 or 202 registers serve nothing), an idle slot is released and **not handed out again before the frames in flight are done** (and comes back after the deferral), GPU read-backs of the uploads 0 differing. `rvk_rhitest --validation` PASS (both backends); `--sync-validation` shows the same 2 old swapchain-acquire hints (frame slot 0 / 1) as 7.36. No shader / ABI change (the skinned permutations already had scene forms: they were built in every run, `variant failed 0`). Game runs in the private copy `risen-test-vk-f6` (instance 38, lock `rvk-f6`), Full + the nine-setting chain + `owned_shadows` + `owned_actors`, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f6/`: **GPU-assisted validation on the short rvkcompare spots (pub_interior + pub_idle, actors shown; first run and again with the final binary): 0 errors, 0 VUID**, morph actors with active weights exercised (18 - 42 per window); `-Validation` savegame hold 60 s: main z 100 %, cascades 100 %, 0 errors; `rvk_rtwalk` from `Risen_Save_0005` (quick-load, walk + turn 12) **walk 1 plain** (`-Validation`), **walk 2 `shadows.enabled` + `shadows.rt=near` + `debug.rt_skin_verify`** (RT skinning of the owned draws: `rt: skin verify` max error 0.0045 units, as with the engine path 0.0044), **walk 3 new game (`load=0`) with `shadows.rt=near`**: all PASS, 0 error lines, 0 VUID, **DIFFERING 0, bone images compared on the check frames (500 - 2,000 per 5 s window) 0 differing**, cascade ops 100 % owned; the 7.35 turning burst (`mode=walk turn=18 keys=TurnRight burst=10 interval=1`) with `-Validation`: PASS, 0 errors; rvkcompare spots `pub_interior`, `hero_idle`, `pub_idle` (actors shown) and `focus=1` (the highlighted overlay on the focused NPC: its ops are composed, the others owned): PASS, 0 errors; `upscale.mode=native_aa` + `upscale.object_motion=characters` (`rt.allow=false`, the owned path is gated off by the upscaler as for meshes, the composed actor draws read the bone table): object motion 68,087 captures / 8 tracked draws off vs 68,625 on in one spot run each; Direct3D 12 spots (`-Api d3d12 -Validation`): PASS, 0 errors (the debug layer without GPU-based validation, `RVK_NO_GBV=1`: 33 old `CreateGraphicsPipelineState` DSV-format warnings; main z 100 %, cascades 100 %, 540 images compared, 0 differing; the GPU-based run of the same spots is dominated by the old `light.shadow trace` dispatch finding of 7.32 and timed out in the pub: not used).
* **Picture A/B** (`set native.owned_actors true|false` every 300 frames inside one hold, shot every 50 frames, `freeze_wait=3200`, `show_actors=1`): pub_interior **toggle pairs mean |d| 0.0014 (max level 2, 4 of 7 bit-identical)** against same-state pairs 0.38 (the pub's fire / noise; 21 of 42 bit-identical). **Motion bursts** of animating characters (rvkcompare `hero_idle` / `pub_idle`, `freeze = false`, the setting toggled every 100 frames, 8 consecutive pictures 20 frames after each toggle, mean |d| between consecutive pictures): hero_idle owned off 0.645 (sd 0.110) vs on 0.655 (sd 0.091), steady within every burst (0.51 - 0.88, no spike, no lag); pub_idle owned off 0.056 / on 0.122 first, **0.117 off / 0.098 on with the toggle order reversed** (the difference followed the time of the burst = the NPCs' activity, not the setting); no pop at a toggle.
* **CPU / GPU** (savegame hold 40 s, lod 2x, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy + default lock, `-Perf`, rounds interleaved off / on / off / on / off / on, the last six 5 s windows of each run; engine + HAL ms per frame): **RT scene on (player defaults): off 8.64 / 8.70 / 8.72, on 8.71 / 8.89 / 8.69 = +0.08 ms (round differences +0.08 / +0.19 / -0.03: noise), GPU 3.240 / 3.253 ms**; **`rt.allow=false`: off 7.64 / 7.59 / 7.21, on 7.56 / 7.12 / 7.20 = -0.18 ms (-0.08 / -0.47 / 0.00), GPU 2.499 / 2.476 ms**. A first build (serve compared and zeroed 3.2 KiB records per draw) was +0.19 ms with the RT scene on; the register-count-bounded compare took it to noise. Per owned op the new work is the stamp (~ 1 us), the image once per part and frame (~ 2 - 3 us) and the persistent draw (~ 2 us): about what the composition (2.3 - 3.6 us per op) cost, so **the CPU gain of owning the actors is within noise; the structural gains are real but small**: the bone images are shared by the views of a frame (the VS object-window ring blocks of the frame drop 587 -> 548 = **-121 KiB per frame**, the bone table uploads 44 - 46 KiB per frame: net **-75 KiB per frame**), the draws leave as indirect buckets, and the actors are no longer composed.
* **Open.** (1) With the RT scene on the solid pass's character draws stay composed (the RT scene's vertex-shader capture of each; a capture-free character path in the RT scene would let them go). (2) The upscaler gates the whole owned path (7.16), actors included. (3) A bone record holds 201 registers = 3.2 KiB for a part that uses ~ 0.6 KiB: a sub-allocated variable-size record would cut the table's upload to ~ 1/5 (the window's `covered` extent is the shader's static read extent, 201 registers; a short record needs a zero tail guard). (4) Per-op reads of the engine for the stamp (no event hooks for actors: ~ 60 guarded reads per op): the ActorInstance's own signals (`UpdateRenderMatrices`' stamp) could replace them. (5) The terrain-map / point-light atlas views have no actor ops of their own in the tests. (6) F6-2 (compute skinning into a posed buffer for RT only, the bone table as its palette source instead of the draws' bone constants) is possible now that the images exist per part and frame: `rt_skin.comp` could read them; not built (the RT skin pass works from the owned draws' loaded registers: `rt: skin verify` unchanged).


### 7.42 F7-2: owned vegetation draws (2026-11-07, branch `worktree-agent-a88891eba3d28dc47`; Debug setting `native.owned_vegetation_ops`, default off, Full only, needs `native.owned_vegetation` + `native.owned_persistent`; settings count 142)

**What was built** (`src/native/static_ops_veg_owned.cpp`, `veg_owned.h`; the F4 tree recipe, 7.25, applied to the grass ops). (1) *Templates.* On composed frames (the check frames) each vegetation op's draws are kept as one template per bucket (type, z / solid, sun bit; table lighting required): the composed draw's retained packet with **stream 1 replaced by one shared static stand-in buffer** (`vro::State::stand`). This matters: F7-1's retained buffers are dynamic buffers, and `Buffer::gpu()` gives a dynamic buffer a fresh ring copy every frame it is used, so a packet bound to them never replays (measured: 23,000 of 24,000 vegetation draws "refused at replay: stream binding" when the packet cache was switched on for the group, i.e. F7-1's "replayable draws" claim did not hold; the group is cached only while this setting is on) and a derivation (`ownedDrawSig`) would change every frame. With the stand-in the template's binding never changes. (2) *Check frames.* The composition is compared with the plan made at its entry (batch count / sizes / bucket keys), with the registers the owned frame will serve (VS window 0 / 1 = the view block's mirror, c31.xy = the fade, c32.w = 1, nothing in c33..c231, PS c0..c4), with the retained templates (`packetSameDesc`, light parameters, material slot; a resource reloaded under the same object pointers with the same shaders / states is `moved`: the template is replaced, not an error) and the retained converted instance bytes with a fresh conversion: `DIFFERING` must stay 0 (a difference turns the stage off), `MISS` from the rolling scrub (`treeTemplate`, now taking its stats and scrub tick as parameters). (3) *Owned frames.* `vegViewBegin` / `vegSelect` / `vegTake` (frame tap: the ops with a valid record leave the engine's lists; the trees' recipe), `vegPass` at `BeginZPass` / `DoBeginRenderSolid`: per op the grid nodes are walked and the source entries read (as `composeVegetation`), `planGrass` cuts the batches (same cursor chain), the stamp (type object, mesh, visual, sub-mesh ranges, shader, buffers, declaration, base vertex, variant epoch, registry generation), view stamp, material poll and texture map are verified, the op's retained **converted instance bytes** (CPU, rebuilt only when the flattened source changed) are copied into ONE ring block in list order, one DrawRecord per (op, lighting entry) is written (VS window 2 = a per-op c31 / c32 block in a ring, base = block - 31 x 16 behind a 32-register pad, PS window 3 = the batch illumination's lighting entry, VS windows 0 / 1 = view slots 12 / 13 with the frame's wind c12) and the whole bucket is drawn with **one indirect command**: `Device::ownedTreeFlush` with the new `VegInstances` argument replaces the template's stream-1 binding (found by buffer + offset in the packet's vertex input; exactly one match or refusal) by the frame's instance block; each position's record comes from the ref list (the trees' per-instance records). 11 commands per flush replace ~58 composed ops with their draws. Side effects the engine slot would run: resources touched (mesh, material, the images the composition collected), the shared buffer's cursor chain (stored before any late composition and at the end). An op that fails any check is composed right where the engine's slot would have run it (`composeLate`). Grass draws get scene ABI v2 forms (`Device::setSceneVeg`; `sceneDecide` treats `Kind::Vegetation` like the trees: v2 only) and are `batchable` while it is on. Records of ops unseen for 1,200 frames are dropped (their record slots return).
* **Deviation from the order: no change to the F2c-4c compaction shader.** Instanced draws need `instanceCount > 1` and a firstInstance: the trees' flush already writes CPU arguments per item (instances = the item's positions, firstInstance = its first list position), and an item is a whole bucket, so a vegetation frame has ~11 commands: the compaction pass would not shrink that, and extending its GPU-address code for it was judged more risk than gain. Open: GPU compaction of the grass bucket arguments.
* **Gates.** `rvk_tests` 320 / 0 (settings 142). `rvk_d3d9test` new case "owned vegetation (F7-2)" (a grass-like instanced draw with per-instance vertex data in stream 1: one item of six positions, three items of two = one indirect call, the bytes in reverse order, per-position lighting records, guards: no instance data, an instance count that is not the positions, a null window base, a template without a stream-1 binding; 0 bytes may differ from the normal draws): PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan `--validation`, `--sync-validation`, D3D12 `--validation`, `--sync-validation`. (The first version of the case caught a real bug: the stream-1 binding search matched by buffer only, but static buffers share one arena VkBuffer, so the stream 0 binding matched too and every flush was refused: now buffer + offset.) `rvk_rhitest`: no RHI change. GPU-address guards: `ownedTreeFlush` refuses before queuing (null instance data, stride 0, > 8 MiB, no / ambiguous stream-1 binding, positions != items' instances, null record windows, record index out of range, slot not live). Game, private copy `risen-test-vk-f7` (instance 39, lock `rvk-f7`, logs `rvk-work/scratch/f72/`): **first run `-Validation` + GPU-assisted** (jungle + harbour_docks, `verify_10`, rt.allow=false): PASS, 0 errors, 0 VUID, no device loss; 5,226 of 5,226 ops owned in a window, 0 late, DIFFERING 0, MISS 0. Soaks on the final build: spots jungle / swamp_camp / lake_jungle / harbour_docks / pub_interior (`verify_10`; 191,500 ops, DIFFERING 0, 0 errors), the three walks (quick-load rt.allow=false 711,986 ops with 10 late, new-game beach 341,978 / 3 late, quick-load RT defaults 709,388 / 10 late), the turning burst with `-Validation` and plain (10 pictures each, 0 errors, 0 VUID), a second GPU-assisted spots run on the final build: all PASS, 0 errors, DIFFERING 0. **Burst / turning: 10 - 12 % of the ops are composed late** (`template refused rc 2`): the draw record table (4,096 slots, shared with the static meshes, trees, actors) is full while turning (`FULL refusals`, identical in F7-1's burst without this stage: a pre-existing capacity limit); a refused op composes and nothing is lost.
* **Picture A/B** (`set native.owned_vegetation_ops true|false` every 300 frames in one hold, a shot per 50 frames, frozen game time): jungle: toggle pairs differ by a constant mean 0.085 (max level 2, 0 pixels over 2 levels), swamp_camp 0.019 (max 2 - 3), lake_jungle 0.000 (max 1); the same-state noise (eye adaptation, water, particles) is 0.002 - 0.05 mean, so the owned grass is within +-2 levels of the composed grass (not bit-identical; not chased: the lighting entry / fade rounding is the suspect, below the engine's own frame-to-frame noise).
* **CPU** (savegame hold, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, Full defaults + owned trees / shadows / vegetation, rt.allow=false, shared copy + lock per run, three alternating rounds): off 7.048 / 7.015 / 7.027 ms, on 6.926 / 6.989 / 6.877 ms: **-0.12 / -0.03 / -0.15 ms (mean -0.10 ms, 1.4 %)**, GPU 2.17 - 2.33 vs 2.35 - 2.37 ms (within the runs' scatter). The vegetation's own timers per 5 s window (about 700 frames): before (F7-1) composition 130 ms (z 61.5 + solid 68.9) + `issue()` 153 ms = 283 ms; after: composition only on the check frames 36 ms + the owned pass 127 ms (nodes 31, source read 10, plan + lighting entries 35, flush + instance block 21, side effects 1) + the templates' record 14 ms = 177 ms: **-106 ms per 5 s = -0.15 ms per frame (-38 % of the vegetation's CPU)**, in line with the frame delta. The pass is dominated by the per-op node walk and `planGrass` + the illumination reads (`vegIllumSun` reads 0x98 bytes per batch): next candidates are a bulk node read (open item 2 of 7.40: not done, the nodes are ~25 % of the pass), a per-pass memo of the illumination reads, and the GPU gather of 7.40 item 3.
* **Open.** (1) The record table capacity (4,096) is the limit while turning (also for the static / tree ops); templates take 1 - 2 slots per op. (2) Node walk bulk read and per-batch illumination memo (the remaining ~0.1 ms). (3) The grass is within 2 levels of the composed grass, not bit-identical. (4) D3D12 game run not done (the d3d9test case passes on D3D12). (5) `native.owned_vegetation` alone (F7-1) still does not replay the grass draws (the packet cache for the group is enabled only with this setting): it remains a no-gain measurement stage.

### 7.43 F-INT: the whole owned chain together (2026-11-08, branch `worktree-agent-a8751cea6c1195160` on main 0a6b4a5; no new setting, settings count 142)
