Every owned stage had been gated alone; this stage runs them together (private copy `risen-test-vk-int`, instance 40, lock `rvk-int`; logs and scripts `rvk-work/scratch/int/`: `run.ps1 -All` = the F1 / F2c chain + `owned_static`, `owned_persistent`, `owned_shadows`, `owned_trees`, `owned_billboards`, `owned_vegetation`, `owned_vegetation_ops`, `owned_actors`, `native.clusters` / `cluster_cull` / `cluster_cull_shadows`, `rt.table_entries`, `lighting.clustered` / `full_lit`, player defaults, `RVK_TABLE_NOTRIP=1`; `an.py` = per-log verdict: every DIFFERING / MISS / mismatch / FALSE NEGATIVES > 0, errors, VUID, device loss, table peaks).

**1. Capacity.** The persistent DrawRecord table (`kCapacity` 4,096) is shared by static meshes (main z / solid + cascades), trees, actors, vegetation templates. Peak live records (the window peak adds the deferred slots: a released slot is held for `kFramesInFlight + 1` presents):

| scene (everything on) | peak live records |
|---|---|
| savegame, turning burst (`rvk_rtwalk burst`), 4 runs (plain, test baseline, 2 x `-Validation`) | 5,640 / 5,831 / 5,928 / **5,938** (145 % of the old table) |
| rvkcompare pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks / monastery_hall / hero_idle | 3,565 |
| savegame hold (RT on / off) | 2,803 / 2,938 |
| quick-load walk / new-game beach walk / walk + `shadows.rt=near` | 3,084 / 2,116 / 1,985 |

The old table therefore filled while turning (7.42 open item 1: late-composed grass). **Now 16,384 slots** (2.76 x the worst peak; 2 MiB of records + 3.3 MiB of blocks). Audit of the places that assume the slot count: record indices are 32-bit in the ref lists (`uint2` slot, view slot) and 24 bits in `VARY14` (`kSceneRecordMask`, 16.7 M); the compaction's `slotCount` push constant is `DrawRecordTable::kCapacity`; `ownedFlush` guards `slot >= slotCount`. **The 16-bit fields are not slots**: `args[3] = rowFirst | rowCount << 16` holds CLUSTER rows (`rowFirst < 65,536 = ClusterStore::kRowCapacity`, `rowCount <= 4,096`: unchanged; a full cluster table is a refusal at build time). A table that grows at run time was not built (a deferred reallocation must keep every in-flight frame's record pointer valid; the measured need is 2.8 x below the new size). rvk.log `native: draw record table (5 s)` now reports `window peak live + deferred N, deferred now N`; `native: owned compaction (5 s)` reports `per-frame peak N of 16384 argument records, N of 4096 counts`.

Other fixed tables (peaks over all runs of this stage): bone table 52 of 1,023 records; material table 204 of 4,096; lighting images 1,712 entries of 8,192; cluster rows 8,674 of 65,536; scene (object) table 5,272 live slots of 32,768; compaction **2,865 argument records of 16,384 per frame** (the cull reserves 8 per clustered record) and 348 counts of 4,096, `frame full` 0 in every run; billboard constants table **2 images of 128** (the "127 records" of `owned billboards` are op records, not table slots; capacity now 256, the table never frees an image, a full table is counted: `native: owned billboards (total)` ends with `constants table N of M images, FULL refusals N`). View table slots (64; the dynamic slots 8..63 of `acquire` are used by nothing): 0 main z, 1 main solid, 2 / 3 `kViewSlotDbvp0/1` (unused), 4..6 the static-mesh cascades 0..2 (7 free), 8..11 the tree cascades (8 + c), 12 / 13 vegetation z / solid, 14 / 15 trees z / solid; 16..63 free.

**2. Integration soak** (all settings above on; `native.packet_cache=verify_10` in the spots runs): savegame turning burst plain x 2 (one with the test-baseline settings, no player defaults) and `-Validation` x 2; spots pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks / monastery_hall / hero_idle (`show_actors=1`) x 2; quick-load walk, new-game beach walk, walk + `shadows.rt=near`; savegame holds (RT on / off); D3D12 spots (`-Api d3d12 -Validation`, `RVK_NO_GBV=1`: the debug layer's messages, 0); GPU-assisted validation runs on short spots runs x 5 (jungle + pub_interior / harbour_docks / hero_idle; the first runs of this stage were plain: each gated stage had had its own GPU-assisted run): **DIFFERING / MISS / EVENT MISS / FALSE NEGATIVES 0, no device loss, no stage turning itself off, 0 validation errors in every run, except one event (finding 2)**. `rvk_d3d9test` (final build): PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan `--validation`, `--sync-validation`, D3D12 `--validation`, `--sync-validation`; new part of "owned persistent (F2c-4a)": every slot is taken (exactly `kCapacity`, unique, one counted refusal), a released slot is not handed out before the deferral and comes back after it, and the case's own draws then run from the table's LAST 16 slots (GPU reads of records at 16,368 and above, through the compaction too).

**Interaction findings.**
1. `native.owned_observe` and ownership exclude each other by design (`FrameTap`: `!observeWanted_` in front of every `ownedViewBegin`): the first all-on run had 0 owned views and no word in the log (the observe stage verifies the engine's whole lists). It logs a warning when both are on now; `-All` leaves `owned_observe` out.
2. One `owned shadows turned off: 3 draws DIFFERING ... vertex input` (a GPU-assisted run, jungle: three Animation z ops of one actor with active morph weights, in a cascade's check frame). Suspected cause, not proven: the stamps / records took the morph pick from a per-instance-and-frame memo (`actorRead`) while the composition (`composeActor`) read the live weights again, so a weight change between the two reads (the engine updates blink / lip-sync weights itself) gave the check two picks. **Fix:** `StaticOps::actorPick` = the one memoised read, used by the stamps, the records and the composition (one pick per actor instance and frame, whatever order the views read it in). 1 event in 1 GPU-assisted run before; 0 in 4 GPU-assisted runs after and in all plain runs. The cascades' check-frame compare of actor ops stays as the net.
3. By design, not bugs: `upscale.mode=native_aa` (with `framegen.mode=fsr3`) gates the whole owned path (7.16, `observers ok false`: 150 of 150 views gated off); that run passed (10 of 10 pictures, no device loss) but exercises none of the owned stages, so the present-order rule of 7.35 is only covered by the owned runs without frame generation. With `shadows.rt=near` the owned trees are off (`ownedTreeObserversOk`: the RT scene captures the trees' vertex shader), everything else stays owned. `native.coverage` blocks the packet cache and with it every record: **a coverage run measures nothing of the owned share** (the share below comes from the stages' own lines).
4. Known, negligible: one static-mesh op per main view is composed late every frame (`replay refusals: texture`, 1 per view): its slot-0 texture is a 1600 x 900 image in `COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL` (a render-target texture shown by a mesh); the derivation refuses it, the composition draws it at its place.

**3. Owned share** (savegame hold, lod 2x, player defaults, everything on; one 5 s window of ~700 frames at ~140 fps; ~80 % of the main views are owned frames, the rest are the `owned_check` frame (1 in 10) and its warm-up frame, composed by design; the shares are of the ops of the owned frames):

| view | draw kind | share owned (rt.allow=false / RT scene on) | what stays composed |
|---|---|---|---|
| main z + solid | static meshes | 80.6 % / 79.6 % of the ops looked at | hardware-batch (instanced) meshes 6.0 % (no per-instance records yet); instance-list meshes 4.8 %; blended / no-z-write draws 7.5 % (F2c-3 exclusion); no record 1.0 / 2.1 % |
| cascades | static meshes | 100 % / 100 % (15 late with the RT scene: an observer wanted a capture) | |
| main | SpeedTrees | 93.9 % / 93.9 % | no record 6.1 % (not analysed: the exclusion counters are in the stage's own lines) |
| cascades | SpeedTrees | 70.3 % / 71.1 % (+ 251 late: stamp) | no record 29.7 % (not analysed) |
| main | grass (vegetation) | 100 % of the ops in owned frames, 0 late | |
| main z / solid / cascades | skinned actors | 100 / 100 / 100 % ; with the RT scene 100 / 0 / 100 % | RT scene on: every solid-pass character draw needs a vertex-shader capture (7.41 open 1) |
| main | tree billboards | 100 % of the recordable ops (1,820 per window) | |
| all | particles, strips, streaks, rain, sky dome / cube, decals, alpha / refracted lists, post chain, GUI sprites, water | drawn by the renderer from natively composed data (`scene_ops`), **not owned** (no persistent records) | their content changes per frame (particles, sky), they are few draws (a frame at the savegame: 5,944 draws, vegetation 2,510, SpeedTree 2,101, static meshes 1,033, instanced 133, billboards 52, skinned 48, decals 23, post 19, the rest < 10 each), or need the engine's state (post chain, GUI) |

**4. CPU / GPU** (savegame hold 60 s, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, three interleaved rounds Wrapper / Full defaults / Full + everything owned, default shared copy + lock per run; engine + HAL CPU and GPU per frame in ms, 5 s windows, the first dropped):

| config | Wrapper | Full defaults | Full + everything owned |
|---|---|---|---|
| RT on (player defaults): CPU | 6.50 / 6.31 / 6.31 = 6.37 | 7.69 / 7.78 / 7.92 = 7.79 | 7.76 / 7.55 / 7.75 = 7.68 |
| RT on: GPU | 4.02 / 4.02 / 3.94 | 3.44 / 3.45 / 3.44 | 3.72 / 3.48 / 3.72 |
| `rt.allow=false`: CPU | 5.55 / 5.40 / 5.59 = 5.52 | 7.00 / 7.10 / 7.07 = 7.06 | 6.93 / 6.94 / 7.17 = 7.01 |
| `rt.allow=false`: GPU | 2.22 / 2.14 / 2.21 | 2.41 / 2.40 / 2.41 | 2.33 / 2.33 / 2.33 |

Everything owned changes the CPU by **-0.11 ms (RT on) / -0.05 ms (RT off) against Full defaults: inside the scatter of the rounds**, and stays **1.3 - 1.5 ms above Wrapper**; the GPU is +0.2 ms with the RT scene on (cause not isolated: cluster copies, the compaction, the RT scene's bulk path are candidates), -0.08 ms without. The owned chain takes ~80 % of the static / tree / grass / actor composition away, but what remains per frame (the RT scene, the post chain, the sprite / effect composition, the engine's own passes) dominates: the CPU prize is no longer here. Open candidates: 7.42 (node walk, illumination memo), the instanced meshes (6 % of the main ops), the cascades' tree parts (30 %), actors with the RT scene.

* **Open.** (1) The morph-pick event was not reproduced either way (one event; the fix is argued from the code, 0 of 4 GPU-assisted runs after). (2) The upscaler / frame generation / GI / G-buffer observers gate the owned path (7.16): the combination everyone will run with FSR is not owned. (3) The record table does not grow at run time (not needed: 2.8 x margin). (4) No picture A/B of the chain as a whole beyond the stages' own: the soak compares check frames, not pictures.

### 7.44 F-VAR: owned draws with the upscaler, frame generation, GI, AO, the G-buffer and the pack variants (2026-11-08, branch `worktree-agent-a5a1094f274930221` on main 46ba956; no new setting, settings count 142)

7.16 / 7.43 open issue: the owned path switched itself off whenever FSR (jitter + forced G-buffer + object motion), frame generation (forces the G-buffer), GI, AO or the G-buffer were on. On the AMD 680M that is the normal configuration (FSR). Private copy `risen-test-vk-var`, instance 41, lock `rvk-var`; logs and scripts `rvk-work/scratch/var/` (`run.ps1 -All -ExtraSetting ...`, `ab.ps1` = one-hold A/B, `an.py`, `cmp_ab.py`, `cpu.py`).

**1. Gate catalogue** (every place that turned ownership off, what each consumer reads of a draw):

| gate | where | effect | consumer / what it needs |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DrawObserver::registersRead()` default `All` -> `Device::ownedObserversOk()` | `d3d9/scene_draws.cpp`, read by `StaticOps::ownedBegin` (`observers ok false`) | **the whole owned path, every view** | `ObjectMotion` (`upscale.object_motion`, forced on by any FSR mode): the object window c31..c37 of a rigid draw + a vertex capture request; `Gi`: ambient / indoor / sun / point-light registers c14..c37 (a per-frame tally); `MaterialRt`: sun (c14..) + fog (PS c0..c4). The three never read more |
| `deriveScene`: `... \|\| gbuf_.active` (reason 4 `variant`) | `d3d9/scene_draws.cpp` | every owned draw refused (composed late) while the G-buffer is on (FSR, frame generation, AO, GI screen probes, `gbuffer.debug`) | the G-buffer's material pass of the draw: `gbufStatic && scopeGbuf_ && lit`, variant `gbufferSlot(false)`; the scene form already existed (`sceneDecide`: `gbufWrite_` -> `sceneSlotOf(gbufferSlot)`) |
| `ownedEnvHash`: the jitter offset | `d3d9/owned_persist.cpp` | the env epoch moved every frame under FSR: every persistent record rewritten every frame | nothing per draw: the v2 shaders read `posFix` (jitter + half pixel + depth remap) from the VIEW block (written every pass); the record's DrawBlock holds zeros there |
| unconditional `++variantEpoch_` in `setGbuffer` (every frame, `Gbuffer::ensureTargets`), `setGeometryLodBias` (every frame, `Upscale`), `setPackBypass` (every frame), `setBillboardFadeScale` (every frame), `setRenderRatio` / `setGiDraws` / `setAlphaDither` / `setCutoutThicken` | `d3d9/device.*` | `stamp: variant epoch` refused EVERY op of every frame (60,228 of 78,072 ops in one 5 s window): even with the other gates open 0 % owned | - |
| the ambient test only for the GI / pack gate (`cd.v.gate && cd.v.staticOk`) | `native/static_ops_owned.cpp`, `_records`, `_bb`, `_trees_owned` (5 places) | `in.ambientLit` stayed false for G-buffer decisions: 8 draws per view refused (`variant`) | the G-buffer's `gbufferLit()` (ambient registers non-zero) from the record's lighting |
| `ownedTreeObserversOk` / `ownedVegObserversOk` | `d3d9/owned_trees.cpp` | trees: an observer that is not `ownedTreesOk()` or reads `All`; vegetation: any observer of the main view's vegetation | GI / material RT observe every kind of the main solid pass |
| `ownedObserve` / `replayOwned` code 13: an observer asked for a capture | `d3d9/owned_persist.cpp` | the draw is composed late (never lost) | object motion (skinned solid draws every frame, moving rigid draws in `all`), the RT scene's characters: a vertex-shader capture |
| `replayAllowed`: `gbuf_.active && !gbufArmed_`; tint / ubershader / `wp0` / custom vertices; replacers that handle the draw | `d3d9/draw.cpp` | no owned draws while the G-buffer is being armed (first frames), or in the debug modes | - |
| `deriveScene` code 8: `alphaDither_` / `cutoutThicken_` with jitter | `d3d9/scene_draws.cpp` | the block holds the frame number / thickened alpha func per draw: refused | `upscale.alpha_dither`, `upscale.cutout_thicken` (Debug, default off): **left as it is** |
| `native.owned_observe` + ownership | `native/frame_tap.cpp` | exclude each other by design (7.43) | - |

**2. What changed per consumer.**
* **Camera jitter.** Already in the view block (`ViewBlock2::posFix`, `currentPosFix`): what was wrong was the env epoch. `ownedEnvHash` now hashes only WHETHER the camera is jittered (the samplers' LOD bias, `jit`), not the offset. Test: `rvk_d3d9test` "owned replay v2" part 1b (a SceneHdr-tagged back buffer + a jitter source; the eight owned draws equal the eight normally drawn jittered draws, the jitter moves 800 bytes of the picture, a slot written without the jitter is refused with code 19) on Vulkan and D3D12, `--validation` and `--sync-validation`.
* **G-buffer / motion / AO variants.** `deriveScene` takes the decision `replayBuild` takes: `c.v.gbufGate == gbuf_.active`, `gbufTaken = gbufStatic && scopeGbuf_ && lit` (lit = the record's lighting for a shader with the ambient semantic, else the Material semantic) must equal the record's `gbufWanted`, and the variant module the draw was offered (`fsAsked`) must still be the one that exists (also in `ownedDrawSig`); the GI decision is skipped while the G-buffer took the draw. `gbufferScope()` is part of the env hash. The pipeline key is the record's own `scenePipeline2` (made by `sceneDecide` at the composed draw: the G-buffer variant PS + the attachments' formats + write mask), so owned draws write the same targets. `CachedDraw::Variant::needsAmbient()` = GI / pack gate or G-buffer eligible: the five places that answer the ambient test from the illumination table use it. The motion vectors of owned draws are the camera motion of the Gbuffer variant (FrameBlock matrices), no per-draw previous matrix is needed; the per-OBJECT motion is the vertex capture path (below).
* **Per-variant epoch.** The setters bump `variantEpoch_` only when a value changes (`setGbuffer`: active / targets / size).
* **Observers.** New `DrawObserver::Registers::MaterialLighting` (Gi, MaterialRt): `loadOwnedRegisters` puts a CPU image of the lighting window into the register file (`LightingImages::cpuImage` -> `IllumTable::compose`, the function that is also the GPU compose's twin and is compared with the drawn registers by the lighting images' own read-back check; `OwnedWindows::Win::lightEntry` set at the five places that build a lighting window); `ObjectMotion` declares `Material` (the object window has CPU images; a capture request composes the draw late as before); `ownedTreesOk()` for Gi / MaterialRt (they tally registers only). `ownedObserversOk` is false only for an observer left at `All`, or `MaterialLighting` without lighting images.
* **Material pack / derived gloss.** Verified, nothing to change: `c.v.pack` is part of the derivation's refusals, the pack word (`packWord()`, incl. `kWordDerived`) is recomputed per derivation and is in `ownedDrawSig`, `packStamp` is in the env hash; `-All` runs with the player defaults carry derived gloss on every lit draw (`pack true` in the refusal logs).
* **Diagnostics:** `d3d9: owned variant refusal site N ...` (first 24 after 4,000 refusals: which of the six variant decisions moved) + `refusal 2 detail`.

**3. Results.** Everything below with the F-INT list (7.43) on, `native.packet_cache=verify_10`, player defaults.
* **Owned share with `upscale.mode=performance`:** before (`observers ok false`, 7.43): 0 % on every view; after: spots 60.3 / 63.6 / 71.5 / 77.9 %, savegame hold 79.6 % (same as without FSR in 7.43: 80.6 %), walk 78.7 %, burst 79.4 %; trees 94 % of the ops, vegetation 100 % of the ops (without GI), shadows 100 %, actors main z 100 % / cascades 98 - 100 %, 0 views gated off, `replay refusals: texture` only (the 1,600 x 900 render-target texture, one per view, as before). With `framegen.mode=fsr3` + `gi.enabled` + `ao.enabled` on top: the same shares (spots 60 - 64 %, hold 79.6 %), `observers ok true`. `env epoch changes 0` per 5 s with the jitter moving every frame; `reused 90.7 %` of the persistent draws.
* **Check frames:** DIFFERING 0 / MISS 0 in every run below (owned persistent, actors, trees, vegetation, shadows, bone / lighting images).
* **Picture A/B** (one hold, `native.owned_static` toggled every 300 frames, 7 toggle pairs vs 33 same-state pairs, mean abs diff; a toggle pair inside the same-state range = no difference beyond noise): pub_interior FSR performance + AO 0.1018 vs 0.1011; harbour_docks FSR performance + AO + GI 0.4712 vs 0.4726; jungle FSR performance 0.61 vs 1.75 (wind); hero_idle (`show_actors=1`, animated) FSR performance 6.04 vs 6.09; G-buffer debug views at `native_aa`: `gbuffer.debug=normals` pub_interior 9.55 (6.8 - 11.6) vs 7.02 (5.7 - 11.6) (the debug view is noisy by itself, ranges overlap), `gbuffer.debug=motion` 0.0000 vs 0.0000 (bit-identical, both arms; the camera does not move in a spot hold).
* **Soaks** (private copy, all of 7.43's settings except `owned_observe` + `upscale.mode=performance`, `framegen.mode=fsr3`, `gi.enabled`, `ao.enabled`): GPU-assisted validation on pub_interior + jungle (`-Validation` + `GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`): PASS, 0 errors apart from AMD's own `rw_output` OpImageWrite format findings of the frame generation DLL (accepted, frame-generation.md 9.4); savegame hold 60 s PASS 0 errors; `rvk_rtwalk` walk 1 (quick-load) PASS, walk 2 (+ `shadows.rt=near` + `debug.rt_skin_verify`: the owned trees are off then, 7.43) PASS; the turning burst plain PASS (peak 5,150 live records) and with `-Validation` PASS (only the `rw_output` findings); spots pub_interior, jungle, harbour_docks, hero_idle (`show_actors=1`) PASS; every run: DIFFERING 0, MISS 0, no device loss, no stage turning itself off. `rvk_tests` 320 / 0; `rvk_d3d9test` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan `--validation`, `--sync-validation`, D3D12 `--validation`, `--sync-validation`. No shader / ABI / translator change (kTranslatorVersion unchanged; the corpus / dxil-check gates are not affected: the shaders are the same texts).
* **CPU / GPU** (savegame hold 60 s, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `upscale.mode=performance`, three interleaved rounds, default shared copy + lock per run, engine + HAL CPU and GPU ms per frame, the first window dropped): Full defaults + FSR performance CPU 8.405 / 8.483 / 8.318 = **8.40**, GPU 2.85 / 3.09 / 3.08 = 3.01; Full + everything owned + FSR performance CPU 8.193 / 7.948 / 8.002 = **8.05**, GPU 2.82 / 2.98 / 3.01 = 2.94. Everything owned saves **0.35 ms CPU (4 %)** and 0.07 ms GPU with FSR on (7.43 without FSR: -0.11 ms inside the scatter): the per-frame epoch bumps and jitter hash were also what made the owned path worse than useless under FSR.

**4. Not done / open.**
1. **Object motion and the RT scene's vertex captures** (7.41 open 1): a draw whose observer asks for a vertex-shader capture is composed late (correct, `late capture requests` counted), so the skinned actors' SOLID draws (object motion `characters` = every skinned draw, every frame) are not owned under FSR (actors: main z 100 % and cascades owned, main solid 0 %). The design for owning them: keep the previous bone palette per part in the bone table (a second image per record, swapped each frame) and let the Gbuffer-motion variant read the previous palette instead of the capture buffer; the object table would hold a previous world row for rigid draws (`all`). Not built: it needs a new vertex-shader variant (`rvk_material_variant`, `kTranslatorVersion` bump) and DXIL parity runs; the table-served draws would then need no capture request.
2. **Vegetation and GI / material RT:** `ownedVegObserversOk` still refuses while any observer observes the main view's vegetation (GI and material RT observe every kind): grass is composed (but replayed from the packet cache) with GI on. They tally registers only; an `ownedVegOk()` opt-in like `ownedTreesOk()` would let grass through, the tally would then miss the vegetation draws (the ambient pick is a frequency vote: needs a picture check).
3. **After a run-time toggle of `native.owned_static`** (A/B): ~8 ops per view stay composed with the replay refusal `variant` (the ambient test answered from the record's lighting is false where the composed draw saw lit registers): the same effect 7.16 noted after Full <-> Wrapper switches (found again: `in.ambientLit false` for the same three shaders with `needsAmbient true`; the illumination table record of the slot after re-enabling is the suspect, not isolated). Harmless (composed late = drawn), only seen after toggles.
4. **D3D12 + FSR + GI + AO + the chain + `-Validation`:** 1 of 5 D3D12 spots runs ended in a device removal (`light.shadow` trace dispatch, DRED `GPU stopped here` in the compute dispatch, then every present failed: the known `light.shadow` finding of 7.32 / 7.36; `RVK_NO_GBV=1`); the identical command repeated passes, and the same settings without the chain / without GI / without FSR pass (`x_dx12_*`: owned records 3,085 - 3,293 peak, 0 errors, debug layer messages 0). Not reproduced, not caused by the owned draws as far as isolated (the failing dispatch is the RT lamp-shadow pass, which reads no owned data).
5. `alpha_dither` / `cutout_thicken` keep refusing under jitter (frame-number input per draw).
6. A unit test for the G-buffer material pass of owned draws was not written: arming the G-buffer needs a real frame structure (SceneHdr role, main view 1, solid draws counted over `endFrame`, transient targets); its coverage is the game's check frames (DIFFERING 0) + the picture A/B above. The jitter case has the unit test.

### 7.45 F-VAR2: 7.44's open issues (2026-11-08, branch `worktree-agent-a61f70db11546c5c6` on main 9f8964e, code commit 5354a3d; no new setting, defaults unchanged)

Private copy `risen-test-vk-var`, instance 41, lock `rvk-var`; logs / scripts `rvk-work/scratch/var2/` (`run.ps1`, `ab.ps1`, `soak.ps1`). Soak setup = 7.44's: every owned setting + `upscale.mode=performance` + `framegen.mode=fsr3` + `gi.enabled` + `ao.enabled`.

**Issue 2 (grass with GI / material RT): done.** `DrawObserver::ownedVegOk()` (true for `Gi`, `MaterialRt`; they read registers only); `Device::ownedVegObserversOk` lets such observers through (`registersRead` must not be `All`, `MaterialLighting` needs the lighting images). `vegPass` shows every owned solid batch to the observers through `Device::ownedVegObserve` (the template's windows with the BATCH's lighting entry as a CPU image, instances = the batch's count, tag = the op's): the same number of `observe` calls with the same registers as the composed batches, so the frequency vote of the ambient tally and the sun / fog reads are unchanged (no tally fix needed). rvk.log `native: owned vegetation draws (5 s)` carries `N batches shown to observers`. Result with GI + AO + FSR + frame generation: grass 100 % owned (jungle: 6,720 ops / 5 s, 9,332 batches shown to observers), GPU-assisted validation 0 errors (only AMD's `rw_output`); picture A/B in one hold (`native.owned_vegetation_ops` toggled every 300 frames, GI on, jungle): toggle pairs mean |d| 2.24 vs same-state pairs 3.79 (wind + GI noise).

**Issue 3 (`variant` refusals after a run-time toggle): found and fixed; not a toggle effect.** Diagnostic `native: owned ambient test false for a G-buffer draw`: `IllumTable::compose` returned false with `why 2` (the illumination's light SET slot not live) for the same few illuminations. Cause: the table's idle sweep freed any light set that no hook updated for 600 frames (a static room's set) while live illumination records still pointed at it (dangling `info[1]`); the ambient test of the owned derivation (compose from the records) then saw zero ambient where the composed draw saw lit registers. The A/B hold only made it visible (> 600 frames). Fix in `IllumTable::flush`: a set referenced by a live illumination is not freed. Result: `replay refusals: variant 2400` per 5 s window -> none (pub_interior, FSR + AO, six toggles).

**Issue 4 (`upscale.alpha_dither` / `cutout_thicken`):** `cutout_thicken` supported: its flag + strength (`kAlphaThicken`, strength x clamp(render scale - 1)) are constant per environment (already in the env hash), so `deriveScene` ORs them into the block's alphaFunc exactly as `draw.cpp` does; `rvk_d3d9test` "owned replay v2" case 1c (alpha-gradient quads + GREATER alpha test, render ratio 2, thicken 1: the thickened normal draws differ from the plain ones by 1,024 bytes, the eight owned draws equal them) PASS on Vulkan / D3D12, `--validation` and `--sync-validation`. `alpha_dither` stays refused (code 8) **by design**: its noise phase (`frame & 63`, bits 16..21 of the same word) changes every frame, so a persistent DrawBlock cannot hold it; supporting it needs the phase read from the view block (shader ABI + translator text change, `kTranslatorVersion`, corpus checks) for a Debug setting that is off by default.

**Issue 1 (characters): RT-scene half done, object-motion half NOT built.**
* RT scene: with the skinning pass on (`rt_skin`, default) a solid-pass character draw that has a skinning job of this frame (the z pre-pass / cascade draws come first) now requests NO vertex capture (`RtScene::observeSkinned`; `setCharacterMotion`, set by `ObjectMotion::apply(skinned)`, keeps the capture while object motion is on, its previous-position chain reads it; `debug.rt_skin_verify` keeps it too); a character without a job (morph weights, ineligible) keeps the capture. rvk.log `rt: scene: characters ...: solid-pass draws N with / M without a job, K without a capture request`. Result with the RT scene on (`shadows.rt=near`, no FSR; pub_idle / hero_idle, `show_actors=1`): actors **main solid 0 % -> 100 %** (1,680 of 1,680 ops per 5 s; z and cascades 100 % as before), 2,358 of 2,400 solid draws without a request, `-Validation` 0 errors, DIFFERING 0; `rt.debug=depth` at hero_idle: the hero and the ground are green (traced = raster) with the owned solid draws. The TLAS geometry is the skin job's output as before (the capture of the same key was only the fallback: `taken` keeps the first entry per key).
* Object motion (`upscale.object_motion=characters`, forced on by any FSR mode): the skinned solid draws stay composed under FSR (actors main solid 0 %, main z / cascades 100 %). **Not built; why:** motion needs the PREVIOUS world position of every vertex in the pixel shader's motion variant. Today that is the capture vertex shader (`cap6`: stores the world position per vertex slot, reads the previous frame's capture of the same draw through `rvkDrawPrevVs()`, a per-draw ring address that changes EVERY frame). An owned draw's DrawBlock is persistent and only rewritten on a signature change; the capture address would have to move out of it (a per-frame indirection table + new scene-form slots for the capture VS and the motion pixel variants, v1 and v2), or the vertex shader must skin with a second (previous) palette. Design for the second: the bone table keeps a previous-frame image per slot (a second table at a fixed byte delta, rewritten from the CPU twin the frame after any change so prev == cur for still parts, invalid after a frame the part was not served), the translator's variant hooks run the skinning twice (the body as a function of the window-2 base), `rvk_prevWorld` from the second run, scene forms `SceneVs` / `SceneVs2` of that variant + `GbufferMotion` scene forms, `kTranslatorVersion` bump, `rvk_shadertool corpus --gbuf-motion` + `dxil-check`, a `rvk_d3d9test` case "owned skinned draw with motion". That is a stage of its own (moving rigid draws in `all` need the same with a previous world row).

**Soak (final binary, private copy, all owned settings + FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO; DIFFERING 0 / MISS 0 in every run, no device loss, no stage turned itself off):**

| run | result |
|---|---|
| GPU-assisted validation, jungle + pub_interior (`-Validation` + `GPU_BASED_GPU_ASSISTED`) | PASS 0 errors (AMD's `rw_output` / `rw_spd_mip` format findings only) |
| savegame hold 60 s | PASS 0 errors; record table peak 1,517; actors main z / cascades 100 %, grass 100 % (3,600 batches shown to observers / 5 s) |
| `rvk_rtwalk` walk 1 (quick-load) | PASS 0 errors |
| walk 2 (+ `shadows.rt=near`, `debug.rt_skin_verify`) | PASS 0 errors (verify keeps the captures; owned trees off by design, 7.43) |
| turning burst plain / with `-Validation` | PASS (peak 5,229 live records); validation: only the `rw_output` findings |
| spots pub_interior, jungle, harbour_docks | PASS; grass 1,924 ops / 5 s owned |
| spots hero_idle, pub_idle (`show_actors=1`) | PASS; actors z 100 % |
| RT scene on, no FSR: hero_idle, pub_idle | PASS; main solid actors 100 % |

Gates: `rvk_tests` 320 / 0; `rvk_d3d9test` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan `--validation`, `--sync-validation`, D3D12 `--validation`, `--sync-validation` (new case 1c in each). No shader / ABI / translator change (`kTranslatorVersion` unchanged; corpus / dxil-check / compare_disasm gates are not affected: the shader texts are the same). CPU / GPU were not re-measured (grass with observers adds one `observe` per batch, the calls the composed path made). Toggling `native.owned_static` (pub_interior, FSR + AO) leaves no refusals now.

**Open:** (1) object motion for owned skinned / moving rigid draws (design above); (2) `alpha_dither` under jitter (phase from the view block); (3) the D3D12 `light.shadow` device removal of 7.44 issue 4 is another agent's task.

## 8. F1 / F2 design decision: windows onto persistent tables (2026-11-06, orchestrator)

**Decision.** F1 does not start with new shaders. Translated shaders and the ported ubershader read every float register through 4 register windows per
stage (`RVK_VSC(n)` / `RVK_PSC(n)`, `shaders/include/rvk_draw.hlsli`; window starts `abi.h kVsWindowFirst = {0, 12, 31, 232}`, `kPsWindowFirst =
{0, 5, 6, 14}`: `c[n]` = `rvkRawLoad(window base + n * 16)`). The window classes already match the tables:

| window | vertex stage | pixel stage |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | c0..c11 view / camera (per view) | c0..c4 camera, fog (per view) |
| 1 | c12..c30 vegetation / tree data | c5 mask reference / obstacle (per draw: DrawBlock `psC5`) |
| 2 | c31..c231 object rows, instance data, bones | c6..c13 material `g_ShaderData` (Material table) |
| 3 | c232.. material `g_vShaderData` (Material table) | c14..c37 lighting (Illumination + LightSet, composed) |

So a window can point at a **persistent table record laid out as a register image** (record address - first register x 16) instead of a per-draw
ring upload, and every existing shader reads identical values: picture identical by construction, verified with the existing `debug.split_verify`
(every window block a draw reads compared with the CPU register file) and the tables' own checks.

* **F1a (CPU path, persistent windows):** for the static ops N5 composes, the windows that a table covers point into the table: PS window 2 + VS window 3
  -> a Material register image (c6..c13, c232..c251), VS / PS window 0 -> a per-view block written once per view, VS window 2 -> a per-instance object
  block (the world rows / fade the composition writes, re-written on the matrix signal or a poll), PS window 3 -> a lighting register image per
  (illumination, pass parameters) composed from the Illumination / LightSet records (first on the CPU with `gpuscene::composeLighting`, then F1b on the
  GPU with its HLSL twin `rvkIllumCompose` in one compute pass per frame). Window bases may lie below the record (base = record - first x 16): Vulkan
  addresses are plain 64-bit arithmetic; D3D12 (heap slot, byte offset) needs offset >= first x 16, so register-image tables start with a pad of
  first x 16 bytes (VS window 3: 3,712 B; a 4 KiB pad covers every window). Gate: `split_verify` mismatches 0, picture identical, the composition
  of the covered registers skipped (CPU measured, gpu-scene.md 9.2 bounds the setter part at < 0.05 ms: the value of F1a is the proof of the register
  images, the CPU gain comes with F2).
* **F2 (GPU-driven):** one uniform shader change for every pipeline of an owned draw: a *scene variant* where `rvkVsWin(w)` / `rvkPsWin(w)` read the 8
  window bases (+ the DrawBlock address) from a per-instance **DrawRecord** selected by the instance-rate ref stream (section 4) instead of the push
  block. DrawRecords are written by the compaction compute from (instance slot, part, material slot, illumination slot, pass); indirect draws per
  pipeline bucket. Translated engine shaders stay valid: batches are still split by pipeline (the engine's permutations), but no longer by register
  values, and the CPU no longer touches the draw. The owned ubershader (graph-shared pipelines, dynamic light loop: 196 pipelines estimated, 7.3)
  is the later step that merges permutations.

## 9. F2c design: DrawRecords from retained op records, no per-frame composition (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

**Hook point (changes 3.2 / F2's "suppress at RequestRenderOps"):** the engine runs `RequestRenderOps` unchanged, appends included (all request-time
side effects stay bit-exact). At `viewBegin` (frame tap, before the sort) the owned ops are compacted out of the db's z / solid lists; an op without
a valid retained record simply stays and takes today's path (composition + F2b-1 scene draw). No late composition.

**OpRecord** keyed (ctx, desc, list, viewKey) = today's packet key minus the draw index, snapshotted from the last composition: draws[] (the
`CachedDraw` packet + matSlot, partSlot, illum info, the shader quad sun x lightmap-combi, scene pipeline), side-effect list (images, lightmap res,
stream 3), flags. Validity = stamp compares instead of `packetSameDesc`: Material version, MeshPart generation, object-table version, illum record
version + light-set epoch (sun bit per set per frame), `variantEpoch` (every setter of GI / G-buffer / pack / tint gates), target-format epoch,
`LightTable::registryGeneration()` (new) for `holdsAll`, a per-illum cached `ambientLit` bit (CPU compose of c15..c21 from the records), per-frame
polls: lightmap residency (res+0x90/+0x98), entity overlay flags (+0x28 & 0xe00000), illum pointer (+0xf8), texture-ready per material.
Owned in the first cut only: mesh ctx, static, main view z + solid, not instanced, fade 1, no overlay flags, no switch-element material, no video
image, no hierarchy node, **table lighting on** (one pass per sub-mesh; without it the pass count follows the per-frame dynamic light count).
Traps: alpha func / ref are not composed (device carry-over) and must be derived natively; observers (RT scene) need the draw: decide before F2c-3.

**Object table** (`GpuTable<ObjectRecord>` by scene-table slot, image c31..c35: c32.w fade, c33..c35 world rows from entity +0x60, base = rec - 31 x 16;
written in the scene-table drain on the matrix signal, fade from the visible list, `checkEntity` on check frames); VS window 1 (c12..c30) in the
view record (+ a `dbvp` c0..c3 variant per view for depth-bias materials). Instanced VS (c32 + 4i) stays on the old path.

**Side effects per visible record per frame** (from the lean path): image LRU touches per visible material, `BindLightmaps(res, 1)` per solid
resource, stream 3 for leftover engine draws, texture `lastUseFrame_`, draw stats. Check frames (`native.owned_check=N`, the lean frame kinds):
no compaction, the old path runs, the record-derived draws are built and compared (normalised fields), not submitted; any diff turns the group off.

| stage | content | gate | CPU (Full, est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F2c-0 | measure: owned-eligible share by reason, observer reads, invalidation rates, CTAB-live c12..c31 of the static VS, occurrence of hierarchy / instanced ops | numbers: done, 7.12 | - |
| F2c-1 | object table, VS window 1 / 2 served, `LightingImages::entryFor`, `LightTable::registryGeneration` (`native.owned_objects`) | split_verify 0, read-backs 0, checkEntity 0 | ~0 |
| F2c-2 | OpRecords + record-derived DrawRecords built and compared while composition runs; read-log signal audit (`native.owned_records`) | DIFFERING 0, audit disagreements 0 (done, 7.14: DIFFERING 0 in every run, 0 - 4 benign audit disagreements per run) | +0.05..0.1 (measured 0.37 - 0.58 ms with the compare, 0.15 - 0.25 ms the derivation) |
| F2c-3 | viewBegin compaction + `Device::replayOwned` at BeforeDepthPrepass / BeforeSolid, engine sort order, side effects, check frames | check frames 0, refcompare Full, long tour (placeholderBinds -> 0), both backends, validation | **-0.5..-0.7** |
| F2c-4 | persistent records, GPU compaction (ref.y = view slot), later cull (F5) | compaction = CPU list | -0.03..-0.05 CPU, GPU / low-end gain |

## 10. F4 design: SpeedTrees + tree billboards GPU-driven (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

Same recipe as F2c (section 9): a retained per-(render buffer rb, type) record + a per-view per-tree snapshot (LOD fields tree +0x48 / +0x50 /
+0x58 / +0x5c / +0x60 / +0x64 / +0x6c, read once per tree per view at `viewBegin`: they are overwritten per db); tree ops compacted out of the z /
solid lists; the CPU writes per frame the instance rows (64 B {c32..c35} per instance, in draw order, VS window 2 base = rows - 31 x 16), one
DrawRecord per instance (template copy + vsWin2 + psWin3 = the instance's lighting image `entryFor(illum, params)`) and one 20 B indirect record per
LOD bucket (indexCount = strip / leaf count, firstIndex = start, firstInstance = the bucket's first row); multi-draw runs through the F2a scene
variant / F2b-1 `sceneFlushRun`. **Per-instance DrawRecords remove the illumination cut (88 % of today's solid tree batch cuts), the light-list /
static-SH / own-lights cuts and the 50-instance limit** (under table lighting; the remaining per-instance permutation input is the sun bit).
Remaining cuts: pipeline (type x z / solid / shadow x sun x material graph / states), the tree resource's vertex / index buffers, topology.
The SpeedTree VS reads its rows as `g_vInstanceData(vInstance * 4 ...)` (TEXCOORD4 = stream 1, ramp 0..n-1 [I]): stage 1 replaces stream 1 by our
own identity ramp (instance-rate, length >= row capacity; `firstInstance` offsets it), later the owned VS sets vInstance from ref.y. Wind c14..c29
(+ c30 fog) in a tree view block (VS window 1), once per view. Billboards: per-(ctx, desc) retained constants images (c32..c36 + c37+j), the easiest
stage. Side effects at compaction: queue stamp reset (rb +0xe0 / +0xe4) + sort (`leanTreeSort`), MaskReference 80 (`leanMaskRef`), material /
resource touches, billboard lightmaps, RtScene observer calls; keep composition for trees while RtScene's animated-tree path (captures) is on
(until F8 supplies the rows). Check frames compare rows / strips / alphas / pipeline exactly; lighting only where the engine's batch had one
illumination (it draws a merged batch with its last instance's lighting; ours is exact per instance: an accepted Full picture change).

| stage | content | gate | CPU (est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F4-0 | measure (`RVK_F4_MEASURE=1`): composeTree phase timers, census (trees / ops / buckets / calls under the new rule, two-bucket trees, no-entity share, sun values, illum classes, hw vs sw instancing), invalidation rates of the tree LOD fields / matrix / illum / rb tables / wind, CTAB of the tree VS / PS (c0..c31, PS c5), stream-1 freq / size / content, RtScene coupling | numbers | 0 |
| F4-1 | billboards from retained records | DIFFERING 0 | -0.15..-0.25 |
| F4-2 | tree records + per-view plans, compared | DIFFERING 0, audit 0 | +0.05..+0.1 (built, 7.25: DIFFERING 0) |
| F4-3 | CPU-owned tree execution main view (compaction, rows, records, args, side effects, check frames) | check frames 0, refcompare Full, jungle spots, rtwalk sway, lod.trees=3, both backends, validation | -1.0..-1.4 (built, 7.25 / 7.25b: -0.22 ms CPU with RT off, ~-0.1 with RT on, GPU z + solid +0.03..0.05 ms, commands <= the engine's batches, RT scene served except animated trees; open: refcompare, rtwalk with wind, picture A/B) |
| F4-4 | tree shadow views (cascades, z) | shadow-compare | -0.15..-0.3 (built, 7.39: DIFFERING 0, spots GPU-AV clean, walks clean, CPU -0.09 ms, noise-limited) |
| F7-1 | vegetation instance streams retained (exact source compare, persistent GPU buffer, replayable draws) | check frames DIFFERING 0, picture A/B | -0.05..-0.1 expected, not resolvable (7.40: the vegetation is per-op cost, 58 one-draw ops per frame; conversion only 7 %) |
| F7-2 | owned vegetation draws (templates per bucket, check frames, pass with one indirect command per bucket, instance block) | check frames DIFFERING 0, picture A/B, validation | built (7.42): -0.15 ms per frame of the vegetation's 0.42 ms, frame -0.10 ms (noisy) |
| F4-5 | persistent per-tree records (UpdateLoD post-hook), GPU compaction | compaction = CPU list | -0.05..-0.15 + GPU |
| F4-6 | owned tree shaders (leaf PS wired: `uberSupportsFile`; vertex pulling; graph-shared pipelines) | equality gates | calls ~10-40 |

## 11. F2c-4 design: the CPU no longer touches individual owned draws (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

Starting point (7.16): the owned path costs ~3.4 us per draw vs ~1.7 us for the packet-cache replay it replaced; F2c-4 must remove the whole
owned path (281 us at the savegame, ~990 us at pub_interior) to pay. The section 9 F2c-4 row (-0.03..-0.05) is superseded.
* **Persistent DrawRecords** (DrawRecordTable 128 B x 64K, slot reuse deferred by frames-in-flight + 1) + a persistent **DrawBlockTable** (208 B
  slots). Per-frame inputs move out of the record: **ABI v2** (`TranslateOptions::sceneView`, own variant slots, default text unchanged):
  `ref.y` = a fixed ViewBlock2 slot (640 B: VS c0..c11, PS c0..c4, VS c12..c30, posFix incl. jitter + depthRemap, PS c5.xyz, flags; fixed slots
  0 main z, 1 main solid, 2-3 dbvp, 4-7 cascades, dynamic slots after); `rvkVsWin(0/1)` / `rvkPsWin(0)` computed from `pc.views + ref.y x
  stride` (positive offsets only); `rvkLoadDraw()` takes posFix / c5.xyz from the view, c5.w = the record's mask bits; VARY14 = (viewSlot << 24)
  | record. Lighting entries pinned by a per-frame `seen` mark + an entry generation in the record.
* **Bulk validity**: a dep table (global env epoch per pass; per entity: overlay flags, illum ptr, object slot / generation; per ctx: LOD
  resource, fade, switch; per resource: MeshPart generation, lightmaps; per material: structure version; per illumination / light set) polled
  once per dep per frame; records are invalidated through reverse lists; per draw a 1-byte valid read. Check frames run the full stamp + read
  log for every visible record (MISS counter, auto-off like DIFFERING) + a rolling scrub of 16 records per frame + a fault-injection debug knob.
* **Compaction** (4c): one compute dispatch per present in the upload list (as the lighting compose), job per (view, list), per (job, bucket)
  stable wave-prefix compaction of the visible id list into args / ref / count in a device-local per-frame arena; bucket = (scene pipeline, vi,
  dyn, ib, frame block) with stable ids + caps; one `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` per hit bucket (new DrawPacket op; zero-filled tail fallback
  without the cap). Order inside a bucket = engine order (stable); across buckets = bucket id (visible only for coincident EQUAL geometry:
  re-run the picture A/B).
* **Observers in bulk**: `DrawObserver::observeOwned(Device&, const OwnedFrame&)` once per frame (views with visible ids + `entered` / `left`
  deltas, per-record immutable views: query, tag, textures, object rows + version, view blocks; default = the old per-id loop). **This is also
  the F8 hand-over** (section 12): RtScene derives its table upserts / drops from entered / left.
* **Side effects batched** per resource / material / primitive per frame (first visible record does the touch), after checking that
  `RequestData(res, 1)` is idempotent per frame (disassemble first).

| stage | content | gate | CPU (savegame / pub_interior, est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| F2c-4-0 | ABI v2 + the F2c-3 owned path on v2; measurements (main views per frame, posFix / c5 per frame, the `submit` split derive / packet / observers, desc vs LOD, RequestData idempotence, buckets, sort share of compaction) | rvk_tests, d3d9test v2 cases, parity_scene v2 == v1, corpus / DXIL v2 | ~F2c-3 |
| F2c-4a | persistent records + dep epochs + CPU-written args (one indirect per hit bucket, exact CPU count) | check frames DIFFERING 0 + MISS 0, A/B, validation, long tour | ~105 / ~400 us (from 281 / 990); **built (7.21): -20 % of the owned path, 231 -> 185 / 354 -> 282 us; the observers + stamps are what is left** |
| F2c-4b | bulk observers (`observeOwned`) + batched side effects | TLAS set equal on check frames, rtwalk, water floor | ~25-30 / ~95-120 us: -0.11 / -0.9 ms vs the pre-F2c replay; **built (7.23): owned path 189 -> 160 us (savegame) / 421 -> 346 us (pub_interior) with the RT scene on (-15 / -18 %), RT observe -0.07 / -0.12 ms, RT pass +0.05; the target is NOT reached (the pre-filter in the persistent loop gives most of it; compaction stamps, build and the 32-frame relearn are what is left)** |
| F2c-4c | GPU compaction + `DrawIndexedIndirectCount` (F5 prerequisite) | rhitest compaction case, read-back DIFFERING 0, sync validation, D3D12 GBV | -10..-15 us CPU; GPU 10-30 us (680M 50-100) **built (7.32): arguments made by a compute pass, `DrawIndexedIndirectCount`; the CPU gain is not there (7.32 has the per-view numbers), GPU +2.5 us** |

## 12. F8 design: the RT scene from the GPU scene tables (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator)

Findings: the tables hold no entity -> parts relation for never-drawn entities (parts are created on first sight; the link is the OpRecord), so
"off-screen" coverage = every entity ever sighted and still cached in, dropped by signals (cache-out, delete, bufferChanged), not by age;
never-sighted ones need an engine visual enumeration (F10, not now). The BLAS key is derivable from `PartCpu` + buffer generations (no strips:
SpeedTree is never a part), so table-built and observed instances share `blasByKey_` (BLAS builds, proxies, budget unchanged). Rows come from
`scenetable::slot(i).world` on the CPU (identity (slot, generation, part), moved = slot.version, no quantised keys). Materials at hits are per
material record except the uv map (evaluated on record change from the record's pools + a representative VS / PS; the table needs a CPU
`Texture*` per sampler slot + the last VS so RtScene keeps its layout / release checks).
* Interface: persistent `RtStaticEntry` (objSlot / gen, opDesc, part / gen, matSlot / structVersion, entity, viewMask, rank) maintained by deltas
  (from `OwnedFrame` entered / left once F2c-4b exists; before that learned from the first full observation); `DrawObserver::coversDraw` pre-filter
  in the observer loops (prepareDraw, replayDraw, replayOwned) skips query building for covered (entity, opDesc); `record()` takes a merged list
  (table entries + registry) through one shared `emitInstance`; no duplicates: covered draws skip observe, a `tableOwners_` guard in
  `selectForTlas`, the observed instance removed on migration. Stays observed: characters (F6), SpeedTrees (F4), instanced batches, grass, glass,
  water, blended layers, fade < 1, hierarchy meshes, everything in Wrapper.
* Risks: parts idle-free after 1800 frames (pin parts with live entries), more BLAS alive (check `rt: scene: lod bands`, cap by radius), uv-map
  animation (dirty only on uv-slice constants), stale addresses in persistent entries (rebuild the BLAS key per frame from buffer generations).
* Verification: `debug.rt_table_compare` on check frames: table set vs observed set (missing = DIFFERING, extra = off-screen, per-pair material
  fields), auto-off; `rt.debug=depth / instances` probes; lamp / RT shadow and water A/B; teleport soak with crash markers; GPU-safety steps.

| stage | content | gain (est.) |
|---|---|---|
| F8-0 | persistent entries learned from observation, rows from the scene table, signal aging, `coversDraw` pre-filter, merged selection, owner guard (no F2c-4 dependency) | -0.15..-0.25 ms CPU |
| F8-1 | materials from records (`RtMaterials::fromRecord`) | -0.02..-0.05 |
| F8-2 | discovery from `OwnedFrame` entered / left (needs F2c-4b) | -0.05..-0.1; **built (7.23): `RtScene::observeOwned` skips covered records by liveness + slot stamp; RT observe -0.07 / -0.12 ms measured (with the pre-filter); the material "ambiguity" of F8-0 did not occur (guard built, never fired): the residual 1 - 7 x 10^-5 material differences are engine-graph state changes bounded by the 32-frame relearn** |
| F8-3 | cascade coverage (after F3 shadow-view records) | the larger share of the 0.376 ms |
| F8-4 | optional GPU instance assembly | only if profiled |
Measure first: observer calls per view / pass / class, observe vs query-building ms, registry composition by class, the share of instances
whose entity has an ownable OpRecord, the cascade-only (off-screen) share.

## 13. F5 plan: measure first, then cluster culling (no cluster LOD) (2026-11-06, accepted by the orchestrator + user)

User decision 2026-11-06: **cluster LOD (Nanite-style simplified cluster hierarchies) is not planned**. Risen's geometry is from 2009, and the
meshes it would help simplify badly: SpeedTree leaves and alpha-tested cards, skinned actors, meshes whose lightmap UVs and vertex colours carry
baked light. RT gets nothing from it on AMD (the caps report: no cluster acceleration structures on the RX 7900 XTX). Revisit only if F5-0 shows
that far, sub-pixel triangles cost real GPU time. **LOD selection itself** moves to the GPU inside the F5 cull pass (per instance: distance ->
the LOD's index range in the indirect args; never in the vertex shader, which cannot change a draw's vertex count). The engine's resource cache
must still see its touches: either every LOD of an in-view object stays resident, or the GPU's choices are read back a frame late and touched per
resource in the batched side effects of F2c-4b, as GD3D11 does for its texture streaming. Measure the memory of "all LODs resident" before
deciding the 680M default. Until F5 the engine's LOD choice stays: it is a dep of the F2c-4a records (7.19 measurement 4: 27 LOD-resource
changes in 2.5 M context-frame pairs).

**Model: GD3D11's GPU world** (`E:\git\github\GD3D11`, `Shaders/D3D12/WorldCull.hlsl`, `WorldObjects.h` `MeshCluster`,
`WorldConverter::ClusterWorldMeshTriangles`, `SpatialBVH.h`). The world mesh lies in XZ grid sections, and each mesh's triangles are split into
~128-triangle spatial clusters by a BVH. The index buffer is reordered so every cluster is a contiguous range (the shadow indices are derived
after clustering, so they stay valid). Per view a compute pass culls the sections, then the meshes, then the clusters (a groupshared visibility
bit mask of up to 2,048 clusters per mesh; beyond that the rest join the last run unculled). A **run of contiguous visible clusters becomes one
indirect command**, so hidden clusters drop out without multiplying draws.

| stage | content | gate | gain (est.) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **F5-0** measurement (no behaviour change) | at the player defaults (savegame, harbour_docks, jungle, pub_interior): triangles / vertices submitted per view kind (main z, main solid, each cascade) and per mesh-size class (pipeline-statistics queries where offered: input primitives, clipping primitives, fragment invocations; otherwise index counts on the CPU); the share of each large mesh's triangles outside the frustum and back-facing (offline from cluster bounds + normal cones built from the mesh arena's copies); a triangle screen-size histogram (a debug pass or offline from captured positions); `-PerfFrame` GPU time of the depth pre-pass + cascades vs the pixel-heavy passes; memory of "all LODs resident" vs today. A 680M run if the hardware is available, otherwise extrapolate from the 7900 XTX and say so. | numbers in 7.x | decides F5-1..3 |
| **F5-1** cluster build | static meshes above a size threshold (level meshes first; set the threshold from F5-0): ~128-triangle spatial clusters (`meshopt_buildMeshlets` or a BVH split as GD3D11), the **mesh arena's copy** of the index range reordered so each cluster is contiguous; z pre-pass, solid pass and cascades all use the same reordered indices, so the solid pass's depth EQUAL stays invariant; per cluster: bounds + normal cone in a GPU cluster table (the GpuTable pattern); cached on disk keyed by mesh content | per sub-mesh, the index multiset is equal to the engine's; picture A/B 0 beyond noise; rt / ocean floor / captures still see valid offsets | none by itself; **built (7.33, `native.clusters`): index COPIES in cluster order (the engine's copy stays), ~130 ranges / 6,800 clusters / 6.7 MiB per spot, 3.6 ms builds on a worker, picture identical** |
| **F5-2** cluster cull (after F2c-4c; **built for the main z / solid, 7.34: 0 - 0.09 ms on the 7900 XTX, cascades not built**) | per view (main + cascades), per visible owned record of a clustered mesh: frustum + back-face cone per cluster, runs of visible clusters merged (cap of runs per record, else the whole range), args written into the F2c-4c arena, `DrawIndexedIndirectCount`; per-instance GPU LOD selection in the same pass (see above) | GPU-safety steps; read-back verify on check frames (a cluster drawn by the engine but culled by us = a false negative, must be 0 for the conservative tests); bursts while turning (no pop-in); sync validation; D3D12 GBV | vertex + raster work of hidden parts of large meshes (680M first) |
| **F5-3** HiZ occlusion | same-frame two-phase HiZ from the main depth pre-pass (section 2's F5 row), per cluster and per small mesh | as F5-2 + pop-in bursts | depends on occlusion (old probe: 8-24 % hidden) |

Only opaque / z / shadow-caster draws are clustered. Alpha-blended draws keep their engine order and their whole range.

## 14. S plan: our own shaders for the owned frame, measured for a bandwidth-limited iGPU (2026-11-08, S-0: measurement + planning, no feature code)

User direction (2026-11-07/08): Full owns the whole frame AND runs our own, optimized shaders; the target is a Radeon 680M (RDNA2, 12 CU, ~100 GB/s shared); the user
tests on it only when everything is done. Owned draws exist for static meshes, cascades, trees, actors, vegetation and billboards, also under FSR / frame generation /
GI / AO (7.21 - 7.45); they still run the **translated** engine shaders (D3D9 bytecode -> HLSL, scene ABI v2 variants). Keep the visual identity (sun flare, exposure,
vivid colours; new lighting adds, never bland / PBR-realistic); Wrapper stays faithful; DXIL parity; no unread outputs.

Setup: private copy `risen-test-vk-s0` (instance 43, lock `rvk-s0`); GPU timing in the shared copy with `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, `-Perf`, `-PerfFrame` with
`RVK_PERF_FRAMES=24` (24 frames averaged per pass), one run at a time. Everything on that the owned chain has: the `-All` list of 7.43 (`owned_*`, clusters, cull,
`rt.table_entries`, `lighting.clustered` + `full_lit`), player defaults, savegame hold (`rvk_perfsave`, lod 2x) and rvkcompare spots jungle / pub_interior /
harbour_docks, 1600x900. **Temporary counters** (not for main): branch `s0-census-temp` (2 commits on 7f4fcc8): `RVK_S0_CENSUS=1` (per owned flush: items, buckets,
distinct VS+PS modules per view kind x draw kind, every 300 frames), `RVK_S0_STATS=1` (`VK_PIPELINE_CREATE_CAPTURE_STATISTICS_BIT_KHR` on every graphics pipeline,
`VK_KHR_pipeline_executable_properties`: VGPRs / SGPRs / scratch per stage, with the module names). Scripts and raw logs: `rvk-work/scratch/s0/` (`run.ps1`,
`queue*.ps1`, `analyze_s0.py`, `vgpr_classes.py`, `perfpass.py`, `mdtable.py`, `merge_census.py`, `static_stats.py`, logs `c_*.log` census, `pf_*.log` GPU).

**Limits, stated once.** (1) The only GPU here is an RX 7900 XTX (RDNA3, 1,536 VGPRs per SIMD lane): VGPR counts come from AMD's RDNA3 compile of the SPIR-V (the driver
reports `sub 0`: wave size unknown); an RDNA2 compile (gfx1035 = the 680M) differs by roughly +-10 - 20 %, and RDNA2's register file is 1,024 per lane: the occupancy
column below is a MODEL (waves per SIMD = min(16, 1024 / VGPRs rounded up to 8), wave32; a wave64 pixel shader halves it). **Radeon GPU Analyzer is not installed
here**: S-2's first act is `rga -s vk-spv-offline -c gfx1035` on the dumped modules (real 680M register / ISA numbers, no hardware needed). (2) All 680M milliseconds are
**extrapolations** from the XTX (the model of 7.22 item 4, front end x5 - 7, pixel ALU x15, bandwidth-bound x10; this section uses x6.5 / x15 / x10); read them as +- 2x. (3) Run-to-run
noise of a pass is large: the same jungle hold gave a solid pass of 1.84 / 1.82 / 1.29 ms in three runs (content phase at the perf frame), so only differences > 30 % are
claimed from single runs.

scene are vertex layouts / index buffers of the engine's meshes, so a z pass issues 82 - 129 indirect commands for 4 pipelines (the command count = vertex input x index
buffer, not shaders). (b) The solid pass owns only ~34 - 80 static items: its other draws are trees, vegetation, instanced, blended / no-z-write (7.43 table).

(a faithful port is not cheaper by itself); (2) **at the player defaults the lit pixel shader is the GI / pack variant at 219 VGPRs (model: 4 waves per SIMD on RDNA2, spills
in 18 modules)**: the XTX is insensitive to it (jungle hold with `materials.derive_from_specular=false` = plain variants: solid pass 1.82 vs 1.84 ms, same scene state), the 680M
will not be; (3) z / cascade shaders are tiny (PS 7 - 15, VS 15 - 17 VGPRs, scene form VS 55 for skinned: bone fetch).
