`perfpass.py`; ms; "post" = the engine's 1600x900 full-screen effect passes with <= 10 draws):

| scene | main z | solid + alpha | cascades (per frame, stride 2) | post (1600x900) | water.rt | water.ocean | rt.scene + light.cluster + light.shadow | submission total |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| savegame (frame 6000) | 0.28 | 0.72 + 0.15 | 0.18 | 0.21 | 1.00 | 0.27 | 0.16 + 0.11 + - | 3.33 |
| jungle (4200) | 0.40 | 1.84 | 0.54 | 0.25 | 1.03 | 0.28 | 0.16 + 0.13 + 0.08 | 5.03 |
| pub_interior (4200) | 0.41 | 1.17 | 0.13 | 0.19 | 0.92 | 0.33 | 0.16 + 0.14 + 0.13 | 3.81 |
| harbour_docks (4200) | 0.36 | 1.13 | 0.12 | 0.22 | 0.83 | 0.30 | 0.19 + 0.12 + 0.05 | 3.59 |

Experiments on the jungle hold (single runs, noise +-20 - 30 %): 800x480 (27 % of the pixels): solid 1.50 (-18 %), z 0.30; FSR `performance` (25 %): solid 1.18 (-36 %), z 0.30,
`upscale.fsr` 0.33 ms; `-NoChain` (translated, not owned): z 0.56, solid 1.50 (z -28 % with the chain: the 600 separate draws against ~80 indirect commands, one run). So on the XTX
the solid pass is **~25 - 45 % pixel, the rest geometry / draw**; the pub (33 + 20 draws, 1.17 ms) is pixel-bound: an interior's lit pixels (normal map + specular + 3 lights) are
the case for the pixel shader.

**14.1.6 Bandwidth** [I: arithmetic from formats at 1600x900 = 1.44 M pixels, not measured traffic; the targets are in `names:` lines]: scene HDR RGBA16F 8 B, depth D24S8 4 B,
depth map R32F 4 B (a colour export of the z pass: read by the shadow mask chain, SSAO, DoF, water, FSR prep), shadow mask A8R8G8B8, refraction copy RGBA16F, cascades 3 x 2048x2048
DF24 (16.8 MB each), terrain shadow map 1024^2 G32R32F, G-buffer normal A2B10G10R10 + motion (4 B each, written only when read: already packed). Per frame: z ~17 MB (depth + R32F
x ~1.5 overdraw), solid ~55 MB (colour 8 + depth 4 + mask 4 + ~15 B of texture traffic per pixel x 1.2), cascades ~50 - 70 MB when drawn (clear + write of 3 maps), **the post chain
~275 MB** (12 full-resolution RGBA16F passes, read + write each), RT / water compute extra. ~430 MB per frame = 26 GB/s at 60 fps (13 GB/s at 30): inside the 680M's ~50 GB/s share
with the CPU, so **the opaque passes are not bandwidth-bound; the post chain is** (275 MB / 50 GB/s ~ 5.5 ms; measured on the XTX 0.19 - 0.25 ms x ~10 - 19 = 2 - 4.7 ms: consistent).

### 14.2 Findings that change the plan

1. **`debug.ubershader` makes ownership impossible today** (`d3d9/draw.cpp:633` split constants off, `owned_persist.cpp:41` / `owned_submit.cpp:39` refuse, `scene_draws.cpp:121` "the
   ubershader has no scene form"): the ported HLSL ubershader and the owned path have never run together. The shader text is ready (`RVK_SCENE_RECORD` branches in `ge_default.frag/vert.hlsl`,
   `shader/uber.cpp:60` emits the define); what is missing is the wiring (variant slot + pipeline key + gates).
2. The owned path's cost is **not pipeline switches** (2 - 20 per pass) but (a) the **lit pixel shader's registers** (105 plain / 124 scene / 219 GI-pack-cluster) and (b) the **geometry
   volume** (2.1 - 2.4 M triangles per frame in z + solid + cascades, 74 % of the solid pass on the XTX) and (c) the post chain's bandwidth.
3. The engine already marks 89 % of the pixel ALU `_pp`: half precision is the shader's own intent, not an approximation we invent.
4. 196 translated pipelines (L1) need ~13 ms of driver compile each on first sight (~2.5 s of worker time, hitches on a new area): the merge ladder (112 / 70 / 57) is a loading /
   cache win, not a frame-time win; with dynamic branches a merged shader allocates the MAX registers of its branches, so merging is capped by classes, not by macro count.

### 14.3 Where a custom shader beats the translated one on the 680M, ranked

Model [I]: 680M ms = XTX geometry-part x 6.5 + XTX pixel-part x 15 (post passes x 10); the solid pass's pixel share 35 % (savegame, jungle; range 25 - 48 %), 70 % in the pub.
Baseline raster time on the 680M by that model (z + solid + cascades + post, no RT / water compute): savegame ~13 ms, jungle ~26 ms, pub ~20 ms (+- 2x). Gains are ms per frame at
1600x900, 3 scenes savegame / jungle / pub:

| rank | opportunity | why on the 680M (numbers) | expected gain (ms) | picture |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Lit pixel shader diet**: real `half` for the `_pp` ALU (RDNA2 packed 16-bit math: 2x rate and half the VGPRs for those values), one lit function with the cluster light loop instead of the 3-light unroll + separate GI / pack / cluster / G-buffer variant modules, dead `PassData` fields out, VGPR targets plain <= 96, lit + pack + cluster <= 128 (from 124 / 219) | pixel part of the solid pass = 4.6 / 9.7 / 12.3 ms; occupancy 4 -> 8 waves for the variant that is the default, 89 % of the ALU in half; 5 fetches per pixel stay (latency hiding is what the registers buy) | -1.2..-1.8 / -2.4..-3.9 / -3.1..-4.9 (25 - 40 % of the PS time) | identical within fp16 (tolerance gate) |
| 2 | **Post chain on own shaders**: fuse bloom / DoF / tone map passes, R11G11B10 for intermediates, half-res where the engine's passes are | ~275 MB / frame, the one bandwidth-bound part: 12 full-res passes -> ~6, -40..-50 % bytes | -0.8..-1.1 / -1.0..-1.3 / -0.8..-1.0 | filter-for-filter port first, then fusion (DoF / bloom identity risk) |
| 3 | **Trees / leaves / grass / billboards on own shaders**: 16-byte pulled vertices, wind in half, cheaper far-field PS, distance thinning (Full may change the picture slightly) | instanced geometry is ~60 - 70 % of the jungle's geometry (473 k of 949 k z triangles), ~50 % of the savegame's (268 k of 775 k) | -0.3..-0.5 / -0.8..-1.3 / 0 (15 - 25 % of the instanced geometry) | grass density / LOD pop must pass the nets / jungle A/B |
| 4 | **z / cascade depth-only**: opaque casters without a pixel shader (the R32F depth map written by one full-screen copy when a consumer reads it), cut-outs with the same texkill threshold, PS-less early-Z | z + cascades = 3.0 / 6.1 / 3.6 ms on the 680M (front end + ROP); PS launch + 4 B export per fragment dropped, foliage overdraw | -0.2..-0.5 / -0.5..-0.9 / -0.3..-0.5 | identical (same triangles, same threshold) |
| 5 | **Command collapse** (vertex pulling from `MeshArena` + one index buffer): indirect commands 163 -> ~25 (savegame), 250 -> ~30 (jungle); VS scene-record reads (+21 VGPR) become a leaner own VS | 1 - 3 us per small indirect draw on a one-shader-engine front end [I] | -0.14..-0.4 / -0.4..-0.7 / ~0 (plus ~0.1 CPU) | identical |
| 6 | Shadow mask filter (post_SSM / enhanced mask 3 draws: 0.08 ms on the XTX = ~1.2 ms) in half with a cheaper kernel at the Low preset | pass is ALU on a full-res mask | -0.3..-0.5 each | PCF look must stay (shadows.md) |
| 7 | Permutation merge (L3 / L4: 197 -> 57 - 70 pipelines), fewer interpolants (6.5 of 9 read; packed half normal / tangent) | GPU ~0 (registers = max of the branches); worker compile 2.5 s -> ~0.7 s, smaller caches, no hitches on a new area | ~0 per frame; loading / hitch win | identical |
| - | not S, noted: cascade budget (map size 1024 - 2048, stride, range; F3-3 cached casters), RT tier off on iGPU (water.rt alone is 0.8 - 1.0 ms XTX = 12 - 15 ms), render scale | cascades 50 - 70 MB / drawn frame; RT passes are ALU | settings / presets | - |
| - | rejected by the numbers: packed G-buffer writes (already A2B10G10R10 + RG16F, written only when read: < 0.05 ms), a custom z pre-pass vertex stream (z VS is 15 VGPRs; vertex bytes ~14 MB / frame) | | | |

Sum of 1 - 6 on the model: savegame -2.7..-4.4 ms of ~13, jungle -5.3..-8.3 of ~26, pub -4.5..-6.9 of ~20 (a third of the raster time; the pixel shader alone a quarter): the first
two items are about two thirds of it.

### 14.4 Stage plan S-1 .. S-9 (Full only; each stage behind a Debug setting, `native.own_shaders` as the umbrella, default off until S-9)

Rules for every stage: (1) the translated path stays as the per-draw fallback for any class an own shader does not cover and for devices without `shaderFloat16` / 16-bit ops
(optional capability with a parity fallback to float), so Wrapper and unsupported hardware are unchanged; (2) both backends: `rvk_shadertool dxil-check embedded | corpus
corpus --scene --uber` 0 failures, `rvk_rhitest` parity on Vulkan and D3D12 (DXC SM 6.6, `native 16-bit` needs SM 6.2 + the options-4 bit); (3) the owned checks stay at
0: `DIFFERING`, `MISS`, `split_verify`, GPU-assisted validation first run (GPU safety rule); (4) no unread outputs: a stage may not add a target / output nobody reads;
(5) picture gates in two tiers: **faithful** (EQUAL: `rvk_shadereq` 183 / 183 + leafs, bit-exact) for porting steps, **intentional** (tolerance + A/B pictures + the
visual-identity check: luma / hue table of `tools/water_stats.py`, sun flare, exposure, colours on pub_interior / harbour_docks / jungle / monastery_hall) for optimizations.

| stage | scope | gates | risk | expected gain |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S-1a** own PS on every owned draw (faithful platform) | wire the HLSL ubershader (`ge_default.frag.hlsl`) as the scene-form pixel shader of the owned path: a variant slot beside `SceneVs` / the GI / G-buffer slots, pipeline key, `uberTaken` accepted by `sceneDecide`, the `!ubershader_` refusals of `draw.cpp:633` / `owned_persist.cpp:41` / `owned_submit.cpp:39` replaced by "uber has a scene form", `uber.cpp` already defines `RVK_SCENE_RECORD`; covers the PS of static meshes, skinned, instanced, trees, vegetation, billboards, cascades (all `PS_Default` / `PS_DefaultZPass`: 175 of 208 requests, 84 %; leafs PS = S-1b) | `rvk_shadereq` / `ubershader_check.py` in scene form (183 / 183 EQUAL, 16 trials), `dxil-check corpus --scene --uber`, rhitest parity (ubershader draw in scene form, both backends), check frames DIFFERING 0, `-Validation` + GPU-AV, in-game A/B `native.own_shaders` toggled (7.44's method: toggle pairs vs same-state pairs), **VGPR not above the translated scene form (124) and `-PerfFrame` solid not worse** | wiring of three gates; the uber is +19 VGPR over the translation today (S-2 pays it back) | ~0 (platform); share of owned PS on own shaders 100 % |
| **S-1b** own VS + leaf PS | `ge_default.vert.hlsl` (proven by `shader_equiv.py --vs`) as the scene VS for static / skinned / morph / SpeedTree / vegetation / billboard (macros ST_*, VEGETATION, BILLBOARD already in it), `ge_default_leafs` PS port (shader-permutations.md 11) | as S-1a + `shader_equiv.py --vs` 17 / 17 in scene form, tree checks of 7.39 / 7.40 / 7.42 (rows, counts, templates 0), nets spots | the tree flush's own templates / ramps (7.25) | ~0 |
| **S-2** lit PS diet (picture-faithful within fp16) | **first: install Radeon GPU Analyzer, dump the modules, `rga -c gfx1035` = real 680M VGPR / occupancy / ISA**; then (a) real `half` in the port (DXC `-enable-16bit-types`, the source's `half` / `_pp` set; specular `pow`, fog, depth math stay float), (b) split ZPass / Default / cut-out entries so no dead code is compiled, (c) PassData slimmed, texture fetches deduplicated where the graph samples one image twice (47 diffuse graphs contain repeats), (d) packed half interpolants | `rvk_shadereq` tolerance mode (<= 2 / 255 on 99.9 % of 8-bit outputs, < 1 % pixels at 2), pictures A/B refcompare Full vs Full (noise-level), banding check on dark gradients (pub_interior, monastery_hall), rhitest parity with the tolerance, **VGPR targets from RGA (plain <= 96)** | fp16 banding / specular sparkle; the driver's handling of 16-bit on RDNA2 must be measured, not assumed | rank 1: -25..-40 % of the PS time on the 680M |
| **S-3** one lit function | lighting in the own PS: ambient SH + sun (+ cascade term from the mask) + the cluster-table point lights (the `RVK_MV_CLUSTER` loop, `rvk_light_model.hlsli`) in half accumulators + lightmap + GI term + derived gloss (`kWordDerived`) computed once; the separate plain / gi / gbuf / cluster / scene modules (5 per shader today: 163 + 134 + 55 modules in the census) collapse into one per class | refcompare Full (own lighting) vs Full: spots equal to noise except the known by-design lamp-light difference (reference-compare.md 11.2); luma / hue table within 1 %; `gi-reference.ps1` quick re-measure; lamp shadows, RT shadows, material RT, FSR + G-buffer variants (7.44 gates) | the interaction of cluster lights, pack word, G-buffer MRT in one shader; VGPR = max of branches: flags only as scalar (per-draw uniform) branches | VGPR 219 -> <= 128 (occupancy 4 -> 8 on the model); with S-2 the rank-1 gain |
| **S-4** classes + command collapse | (a) merge permutations to the L3 / L4 classes (z / cascade: static, skinned, morph x opaque / masked; lit: static / skinned / tree / leaf / vegetation x opaque / alpha) with record flags (light counts, normal map, specular, lightmap, cut-out as uniform branches), graph forms as a small recipe table; (b) vertex pulling from `MeshArena` (no vertex input state, one index buffer; D3D12 `SV_VertexID` base-vertex rule: `vertexOffset = 0`, absolute indices) | `bucket key clash` 0, rhitest `testIndirectPull`, pipelines per frame and compile time logged (`d3d9: pipelines built`), commands per frame (82 / 29 / 41 -> single digits per view), picture identical (same triangles), shadereq on every class | VGPR growth by merging: keep a class when max VGPR rises > 10 % | rank 5 (-0.14..-0.7 ms) + the loading win of rank 7 |
| **S-5** vegetation / trees / billboards | own SpeedTree / grass / billboard VS + PS: 16-byte pulled vertices, half wind, far-field grass PS, distance thinning tied to `lod.view`; intentional picture change (small) | nets (`net8_*`), jungle / swamp A/B + motion bursts (upscaling.md 12 flicker), `rvk_rare` billboards, F7's 7.40 / 7.42 checks | pop-in of thinned grass (burst test), FSR flicker | rank 3 (jungle -0.8..-1.3) |
| **S-6** depth-only z / cascades | PS-less opaque z in the cascades and the main pass; the R32F depth map by a copy only while a consumer reads it; cut-out variants keep the texkill threshold; cluster cull in the cascades if still off (F5 follow-up) | `shadow-compare.ps1` (engine vs enhanced cascades), the R32F consumers (FSR prep, SSAO / DoF, water, shadow mask chain) pixel-equal, `rvk_d3d9test` depth cases, no-stencil rule | the consumers of the depth map that read it mid-pass | rank 4 (-0.2..-0.9) |
| **S-7** post chain on own shaders | the engine's effect filters (native post, engine-frame/README.md 15, `native/effect_ops_post.cpp`) re-written filter-for-filter in HLSL (EQUAL first), then fused (bloom + DoF + tone map) with R11G11B10 intermediates | per filter EQUAL (the post units' compare), then refcompare post spots, exposure / adaptation identical, sun flare / streaks unchanged | DoF / bloom identity (visual identity is exactly here), FSR / frame-generation input order | rank 2 (-0.8..-1.3) |
| **S-8** shadow mask + iGPU preset | own mask filter in half, a "680M" quality preset (cascade size 1024 / 2048, stride, range, RT off, render scale) from the measured table | shadows.md A/B, `water-compare`-style spots, preset table in the overlay | settings audit (settings-audit.md) | rank 6 + the non-S rows |
| **S-9** default on + the 680M day | `native.own_shaders` default on in Full (translated fallback kept), RGA numbers of the final modules, the user's 680M test with the preset; update PLAN.md / CLAUDE.md | all gates above green on Vulkan + D3D12, the soak of 7.43 (all settings on) with the own shaders | the real hardware differs from the model | the sum of 1 - 7 |

Order: S-1a (platform, 0 gain, unblocks everything) -> S-2 + S-3 (the two thirds; start with RGA so the targets are real) -> S-1b -> S-4 -> S-5 / S-6 (independent) -> S-7 -> S-8 -> S-9.
S-1a is first because it turns the owned path onto our own pixel shader for **all owned draw kinds at once** (every material PS of static, skinned, instanced, trees, vegetation and
billboards is the same `PS_Default` text): up to ~4,500 of the 5,944 draws of a savegame frame (76 %: vegetation 2,510, trees 1,282, static 640, skinned 32, billboards 52) are owned, and the lit pixel shader is where the 680M's time is.

### 14.5 Not measured / open

1. No RDNA2 compile and no 680M run exist: the occupancy column and every 680M ms are models (install RGA first; the user's hardware test is the final check). Wave size of the
   driver's pixel shaders (32 or 64) is unknown (`sub 0`): a wave64 compile halves every occupancy figure above.
2. The solid pass's pixel / geometry split is bracketed (25 - 48 %) by two noisy resolution experiments; a per-draw-kind GPU timing (veg / trees vs static) was not taken (the cap
   of the timestamps is the pass).
3. The trees / vegetation / billboard flushes were not given VS / PS module census lines (only the static + skinned `ownedFlush`); their pipelines appear in the pipeline
   statistics (412 PS modules) but are not attributed per flush.
4. `materials.derive_from_specular` off vs on shows no XTX difference (1.82 vs 1.84 ms): the 219-VGPR variant's real cost is a 680M question (RGA).
5. Fp16 on RDNA2 through DXC / SPIR-V (`RelaxedPrecision` vs native `float16_t`) and through DXIL min16float on D3D12 must be measured in S-2 (gain assumed 2x rate on packed ops).
6. The cascade budget, F3-3 and the RT tier presets are not S items but are larger than ranks 4 - 7 on the 680M (cascades 50 - 70 MB / drawn frame, `water.rt` 12 - 15 ms by the model).

### 14.6 S-1a result: the HLSL ubershader as the scene-form pixel shader of owned draws (2026-11-08, code commit 5526097 on main f296100; no new setting, settings count unchanged, defaults unchanged)

**Setting: `debug.ubershader` is reused** (Debug, default off), not a new `native.own_shaders`: the switch already means "material pixel shaders from the HLSL port, translated fallback for what it cannot build", the owned path needed only the refusals removed, and the umbrella `native.own_shaders` of 14.4 is a S-9 decision (one more setting would also touch `tests/settings_api_tests.cpp`'s count for no gain now). The setting now also works at run time (A/B F10, `-Commands 'N: set debug.ubershader true'`): `Device::resetUberVariants` drains the variant workers and forgets the variants of every material pixel shader (old modules live on in pipelines); retained packets / owned derivations notice through `CachedDraw::Variant::uberAsked`.

**What changed.** (1) `draw.cpp`: split constants no longer switch off for the ubershader (it reads c# through `RVK_PSC` windows like a translated shader; its reads are a subset of the translated shader's: the unused `RVK_LOAD_SHADER_DATA` loads are dead-code-eliminated, so the reflection's window extents suffice), `replayAllowed` no longer refuses it, a draw that took the ubershader module (or a variant) is retained (`captureOk_`), the plain module is requested only for draws no GI / pack / G-buffer variant is wanted for (the variants are ubershader modules by themselves: their providers already set `TranslateOptions::uber`; 148 needless plain modules less per run). (2) `owned_persist.cpp` / `owned_submit.cpp`: the `ubershader_` refusals are gone. (3) `scene_draws.cpp`: `uberTaken` no longer refuses the scene form; the plain scene slots (`ScenePs` / `ScenePs2`) are built from the ubershader (`variantWorker`: `uberBaseOptions` when the request is covered). `variantModule` falls back to the translated scene form when the ubershader build fails (`uberFallbacks_`). (4) Counters: rvk.log `native: owned shaders (5 s)` = per draw kind, owned items and scene-form decisions by pixel shader form: uber / other (not a material shader) / leaf (S-1b) / class skipped (`debug.ubershader_skip`) / request unknown / build failed.

**Gates.**
* Shader equivalence in scene form: `python tools/ubershader_check.py out/matsrc/*.jsonl [--scene | --scene2]` (new: `rvk_shadereq --scene / --scene2` builds one DrawRecord (+ ViewBlock2) from the random constants; the translated side = `rvk_shadertool export corpus game-shaders-scene --scene` / `game-shaders-scene2 --scene2`; `test/eq_varyings.vert` with `RVK_SCENE_RECORD` [+ `RVK_SCENE_VIEW`]): plain 259 EQUAL, scene v1 259 EQUAL, scene v2 259 EQUAL (63 NO-HLSL: not in this corpus export, 4 unsupported entries; the earlier 183 was a smaller capture). Variants (GI / G-buffer / cluster) are not compared by `shadereq` (they need a frame block and extra targets); they are covered by rhitest (hooks) + dxil-check + the in-game check frames and A/B.
* `rvk_rhitest` scene cases (new: z pass masked, G-buffer + GI hooks, v1 and v2) on Vulkan and D3D12 with `--validation` and `--sync-validation`: all `scene variant (...)` PASS, 0 differ. The one Vulkan `--sync-validation` finding (swapchain image WRITE_AFTER_READ vs `vkAcquireNextImageKHR` in the harness swapchain, 2 errors) is **pre-existing** (identical on main's binary).
* `rvk_shadertool dxil-check uber out/matsrc/*.jsonl` (337 requests x 8 variants incl. scene v1 / v2): 2,696 of 2,696 pass. `rvk_tests` 321 / 0.
* **VGPRs** (RDNA3 compile, pipeline statistics, 3 spots, `-All`, player defaults; same hash x variant, translated -> ubershader): lit PS_Default **scene v2 (the owned form) 117.6 -> 117.4**; scene v1 (composed scene draws) 129.7 -> 138.0 (+8, not on the owned path); z pass scene v2 17.4 -> 19.3, v1 13.8 -> 17.0 (tiny absolute values); **the GI / pack / cluster variants that the player defaults run on lit draws: scene v2 248.0 -> 194.7, scene v1 250.7 -> 192.2, plain 198.8 -> 140.9** (the 10 - 16 spilling variant modules per class no longer spill). The owned lit form is not above the translated one, the default lit form is 22 - 29 % smaller; the increases are the v1 plain form (+6 %) and the z pass (+2 VGPR). (The pipeline-statistics hook was a temporary patch from `s0-census-temp`, reverted, not committed.)
* Check frames: DIFFERING 0 / MISS 0 in every run below.

**Share of owned items on the ubershader** (rvk.log, last window of each run; `leaf` = `ge_default_leafs.fx`, S-1b): savegame hold (all owned settings, FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO): static meshes 89,373 of 89,373 (100 %), skinned 2,833 / 2,833, vegetation 6,960 / 6,960, SpeedTrees 23,087 of 37,727 (61 %; the other 14,640 are leaf pixel shaders); walk: static 100 %, skinned 100 %, trees 23,868 / 38,028 (63 %), vegetation 100 %; turning burst: static 100 %, trees 23,439 / 34,143 (69 %); spots: static 100 %. Nothing else falls back: "other / skipped / unknown / failed" are 0 everywhere, `translated builds after an ubershader failure 0`. The owned share of ops is unchanged by the switch (pub_interior 63.8 %, jungle 78.6 %, harbour_docks 66.9 %, monastery_hall 69.8 %).

**Pipelines / buckets / commands before -> after** (pub_interior + jungle + harbour_docks, `-All`): buckets = indirect packets 64,200 -> 64,200, items 177,000 -> 177,000, scene indirect calls per window 6,480 -> 6,480: **identical** (a bucket's key = vertex input x dynamic state x index buffer x pipeline; the pipeline set did not change). Pixel-shader modules built over the run: 432 -> 579 before the "no plain module for variant draws" fix (148 extra), 350 / 400 in the final soaks (hold / 4 spots), 0 failures; 350 modules take 8.7 s on the three workers with a warm SPIR-V cache (cold DXC ~40 - 60 ms each, as for the translated ones). A run-time toggle resets ~120 pixel shaders and the owned share is back within one 5 s window.

**Picture A/B in one hold** (`debug.ubershader` toggled every 300 frames, 7 toggle pairs vs 33 same-state pairs, mean absolute difference per pixel, `cmp_ab.py`): pub_interior FSR performance 0.149 vs 0.817 (floor 0.127); jungle FSR performance 3.59 vs 4.01; harbour_docks FSR + GI + AO 0.461 vs 0.560; monastery_hall FSR + GI + AO 0.322 vs 1.057; pub_interior FSR + GI + AO 0.153 vs 0.933; jungle without FSR 1.59 vs 4.76 (wind): every toggle pair is inside the same-state range (noise = eye adaptation, wind, jitter), none bit-identical on either side.

**GPU per pass** (XTX, `-PerfFrame` 24-frame mean, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy; solid / main z, ms; single runs, noise +-20 - 30 %): savegame hold 0.790 -> 0.824 / 0.256 -> 0.243, total 3.652 -> 3.716; jungle solid 1.827 -> 1.749, total 5.921 -> 5.791; pub_interior solid 0.442 -> 0.435 (total 2.139 -> 2.291 is `light.shadow` 1.18 -> 1.30, not an owned pass); harbour_docks solid 0.472 -> 0.519 (+ mid 0.580 -> 0.603), total 3.201 -> 3.308. Not worse beyond noise on the XTX (the register saving is a 680M question). **CPU** (savegame hold, three interleaved rounds, shared copy, engine + HAL ms per frame): off 7.77 / 7.52 / 7.49, on 9.54 (one outlier window, sd 2.2) / 7.43 / 7.91: no difference beyond noise; the packet cache replays 97 % in both.

**Soaks** (private copy `risen-test-vk-s1`, instance 44, lock `rvk-s1`; all owned settings + `debug.ubershader` + FSR performance + frame generation + GI + AO): first run `-Validation` + GPU-assisted on pub_interior (0 errors); then GPU-assisted spots pub_interior + jungle PASS (only AMD's frame-generation `rw_output` format findings, 10 errors / 12 VUID lines as in 7.44); savegame hold PASS 0 errors; `rvk_rtwalk` walk 1 PASS 0; walk 2 (+ `shadows.rt=near`, `rt_skin_verify`) PASS 0; turning burst plain PASS (peak 5,146 live records), with `-Validation` PASS (only the `rw_output` findings); spots pub_interior / jungle / harbour_docks / monastery_hall PASS; hero_idle + pub_idle PASS. No device loss, no stage turned itself off.

**Open.** (1) SpeedTree leaf pixel shaders (39 % of owned tree items) stay translated until S-1b (`uberSupports` excludes `ge_default_leafs.fx`; the port exists: `uberSupportsFile`). (2) Variants are checked by rhitest / dxil-check / in-game only, not by `shadereq`. (3) The lit v1 plain form is +8 VGPR, the z pass +2: S-2's diet should start from the v2 numbers. (4) No D3D12 game run was made (rhitest parity on D3D12 only). `rvk_d3d9test` PASS 0 failures / 0 validation errors on Vulkan and D3D12, `--validation` and `--sync-validation`.

### 14.8 S-7 result: the post chain on our own shaders - the catalogue, and the luminance chain as two dispatches (2026-11-08; code eb011ed + the fixes after it; setting `native.own_post` = off | on | compare, Debug, Full, default off; settings count 143)

**The catalogue overturns the S-0 premise.** The post chain is **not** bandwidth-bound and is not "12 full-resolution RGBA16F passes, ~275 MB". Frame 2500 of the savegame hold (`-LogFrame`, 1600x900, the engine's default DoF graph, 19 effect draws,
every target logged) and the shaders' tap lists (`rvk_fxtool dump --listing` of the corpus's post_hdr / post_bloom / post_dof / post_streaks variants):

| # | unit (filter : technique) | target | reads | bytes (read + write) | GPU, XTX (ms) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Down64x64 : LogLuminance | 64x64 R32F | scene RGBA16F, 4 point taps per texel (16 K taps) | 0.13 MB + 16 KB | 0.005 |
| 2 - 6 | Down32x32 .. Down2x2 : DownSample | 32 .. 2 R32F | previous level, 4 point taps | < 20 KB each | 0.003 - 0.004 each |
| 7, 8 | AdaptiveLiminance, AvgLuminance : Copy | 1x1 R32F (the persistent adapted luminance) | 2x2 level, the previous value | ~0 | 0.004 each |
| 9 | BrightPass (BrightPassAlphaMask) | 309x210 A8R8G8B8 | scene 4 taps / texel, depth map, avg luminance | 2.4 + 0.26 MB | 0.010 |
| 10 - 12 | GlowH, Streak (64 radial taps), GlowVAddBloom2 | 309x210 A8R8G8B8 | bloom images (cache-resident) | ~0.5 MB each | 0.006 / 0.012 / 0.006 |
| 13 | ToneMap | **1600x900 A8R8G8B8** (not FP16) | scene | 11.5 + 5.8 MB | 0.013 |
| 14 | DoFDown | 400x225 A8R8G8B8 | ToneMap (4 taps = all of it) + depth map (16 taps = all of it) | 11.5 + 0.36 MB | 0.011 |
| 15 - 18 | BlurH, BlurV, CalcNearCoc, SmallBlur | 400x225 A8R8G8B8 | the 400x225 images | ~0.7 MB each | 0.005 each |
| 19 | DoF (screen) | back buffer X8R8G8B8 | ToneMap 5 taps, depth map, blur images, bloom | 12 + 5.8 MB | 0.030 |

Total **~48 MB** per frame (6x less than 14.1.6's 275 MB: every intermediate is 8 bit or a small R32F; only ToneMap / DoFDown / DoF touch full-resolution images) and **0.15 - 0.17 ms** of GPU time on the XTX (19 passes; `postsum.py`
over `-PerfFrame` logs): the three full-resolution passes are 0.054 ms (36 %), the luminance chain 0.030 - 0.046 (20 - 27 %), the bloom chain 0.033 (22 %), the DoF middle 0.019 (12 %), pass barriers the rest. On the XTX the chain is
**per-pass overhead, not bandwidth** (3 - 6 us for a 16 KB pass); on the 680M the 48 MB are ~0.5 - 0.8 ms of its 50 - 100 GB/s plus the same per-pass overhead x ~3 - 5 [I, not measured]. Consequences for the plan: "R11G11B10 / half-res
intermediates" gains nothing (they are already 8 bit); folding ToneMap into DoFDown + DoF saves ~13 % of the bytes (the scene is re-read as RGBA16F instead of the 8-bit ToneMap image) and a quantisation step; the real saving is **fewer passes**
(19 -> ~7 possible: luminance 8 -> 1 (built), bloom 4 -> 2, ToneMap folded in, DoF middle 4 -> 1), and S-7's -0.8..-1.3 ms of 14.3 is an overestimate: ~-0.2 .. -0.4 ms on the 680M by the pass-count model.

**What is built (group 1: the luminance chain, 8 passes -> 2 dispatches).** `native.own_post` (Full; `on` needs the post chain taken over, `compare` needs it composed): the eight luminance units of the scene graph are recorded as `EffectOps`
composes them (the composed draws are the source of every parameter: the quad's uv at the sample points, the taps' offsets, the adaptation constants c1.x lerp factor and c0.xy AvgLumClamp, the textures, the targets), held (not drawn), and
when the eighth is composed `native/own_post.cpp` records two compute dispatches (`Device::runPassNow`, new: an ad-hoc pass mid-frame, the body of `inject` for one pass): `post_lum_tile.comp.hlsl` (64 groups of 8x8: the 64x64 level straight
from the scene with the engine's tap order, then 2x2 boxes down to one texel of the 8x8 level) and `post_lum.comp.hlsl` (one group: levels 4, 5, the geometric mean, the lerp with the previous value, the clamp) which writes the Adaptive
unit's 1x1 target and the persistent AvgLuminance image (`Texture::setSmallStorage`: R32F render targets <= 64x64 get storage usage while the setting is on; Vulkan's storage view of the swizzled (r, 1, 1, 1) R32F image is its identity view,
fixed in `rhi/vulkan`). The previous value rides to dispatch B in row 8 of the tile image (the output is also the source: no SRV / UAV aliasing, found by D3D12 GPU-based validation). The tile stages need the engine's DownSample taps to be aligned 2x2
blocks (checked per run). A group that breaks off (a unit fails composition, another unit arrives) or a kernel that cannot run (pipeline not built, tap geometry not as expected, textures without storage, FSR frames where the post chain sees
the display-size image but no substitute is set: ~1 - 3 % of the frames) **flushes the held units as draws** at that point, in order: the picture is then the engine's. Findings that cost a run each: the renderer shifts every D3D9 draw by the half
pixel (`posFix` = (+1/W, -1/H) in clip space), so D3D9 pixel p samples the quad at NDC 2p/W - 1, not at the pixel centre (the first kernel with centre sampling was 0.15 % off); with FSR the post chain's scene texture is the upscaler's output (taps 1/1600
while the engine image is 1066x600): the kernel samples the substitute's slot.

**Gates.**
* `rvk_tests` 321 / 0. `dxil-check embedded`: post_lum.comp and post_lum_tile.comp PASS. `rvk_rhitest` case "post luminance chain by compute" (new, `tools/rhitest/parity_post.inl`: an RGBA16F scene through the bindless heap, the parameter block by reference,
  two dispatches, two storage outputs; CPU port of the eight draws; the backends cross-checked): PASS on Vulkan and D3D12 with `--validation` (rel difference to the CPU 0, Vulkan vs D3D12 0) and `--sync-validation` (only the two swapchain-acquire
  WRITE_AFTER_READ hazards every Vulkan `--sync-validation` run of this tool prints, also with no case selected).
* **Picture equality, in the game, numerically** (`native.own_post=compare`: the kernel next to the engine's units, before the Copy overwrites the previous value; read back and compared with the Adaptive unit's target): relative difference
  **max 3.9e-5, mean 1.7e-6** over 4,600 frames (pub_interior + jungle; 0.1 % of the frames above 1e-5). Tolerance: the luminance divides ToneMap's exposure, 4e-5 relative = 0.004 % brightness, a hundredth of one 8-bit step; the rest is fp32
  summation order and the engine's `_pp` taps.
* ON vs OFF pictures (`water-compare -Mode full`, frozen spots; control = off vs off, `tools/sbs_diff.py`): mean |d| ON 0.72 / 1.36 / 1.55 / 2.85 / 3.90 / 0.08 (pub_interior, monastery_hall, harbour_docks, jungle, beach_sun, sea_horizon) vs control
  0.47 / 1.48 / 0.20 / 3.68 / 2.48 / 0.51; luma differences inside the control's. `shot-reviewer` (crops, radial glow profiles of beach_sun / sea_horizon, a 12-cell grid of harbour_docks): no exposure, bloom, glare or colour change beyond the control
  (harbour_docks' +2.4 % luma = one splash particle on one side); the hard sun disc is not in frame in beach_sun, the flare is judged through the horizon glow (sea_horizon identical: 0.08).
* Game runs, private copy `risen-test-vk-s7` (instance 45, lock `rvk-s7`), Full + `native.own_post=on` + `native.scene_ops_compare` + `native.lean_check=10` (the held units' compositions compared with the engine's draws on check frames): first run
  `-Validation` + GPU-assisted (pub_interior): **0 messages**; FSR quality + `-SyncValidation` (harbour_docks, jungle): only AMD's own `rw_spd_mip3` format warning; FSR quality + frame generation (savegame hold): PASS; **D3D12** (`-Api d3d12 -Validation`,
  `RVK_NO_GBV=1`): only the DSV-format warning the baseline prints; with GPU-based validation the aliasing described above was found and fixed (3,708 reports, 0 after); turning burst (`rvk_rtwalk` burst, savegame, `-Validation`): 0 messages, check
  frames `after 0 / 43,337 differing, store 0 / 43,337, fields 0 / 87,034`, composition mismatches 0; the walk ran before the last fixes (storage-view VUID only, fixed since). Defaults unchanged (off), `Debug` setting.

**GPU per pass** (XTX, `-PerfFrame` 24-frame means, `RisenTest.exe`, vsync off, shared copy, `-All`; three runs each, `postsum.py`; the post range = first luminance pass .. DoF screen pass): savegame hold off 0.170 / 0.181 (a third log without a post range)
-> on 0.151 / 0.130 / 0.202 (one spiked frame, min 0.102); harbour_docks off 0.140 / 0.115 / 0.208 (spike) -> on 0.113 / 0.110 / 0.110; the luminance part: eight draws 0.030 - 0.046 ms -> `post.lum` (both dispatches) **0.010 ms**; passes in the range 19 -> 12.
Net **-0.02 .. -0.03 ms of 0.15 - 0.17** (about -15 %, of ~3.5 ms per frame < 1 %): inside the run-to-run noise of a single run, consistent across the six. A first version with ONE workgroup was 0.035 ms (slower than the draws: 16 K dependent point fetches on one CU),
hence the tile dispatch. Bytes per frame ~48 -> ~47.7 MB (the luminance units are 0.3 MB): unchanged. 680M extrapolation (pass-count model, per-pass overhead 3 - 5x the XTX's, kernel time x ~3): -7 passes x ~15 - 25 us - ~30 us = **-0.07 .. -0.15 ms**
[I, unmeasured].

**Not built (S-7 remains open).** Bloom (BrightPass + GlowH + Streak + GlowV: 4 -> 2 with the streak's 64 radial taps), the ToneMap fold into DoFDown / DoF (needs inline tonemapping of 4 + 5 taps and a quantisation decision: the engine's ToneMap image is 8 bit), the DoF middle
(BlurH / BlurV / NearCoc / SmallBlur -> 1 group per tile with halos), and the HDRI-only graph's other filters; each is a port from the translated shaders (listings: `rvk_fxtool dump --listing`) + a compare mode like this one. By the table above the remaining
gain on the XTX is ~0.05 - 0.08 ms and on the 680M ~0.2 - 0.3 ms: worth doing for the iGPU target, not a large share of the frame; S-8 (shadow mask, iGPU preset) and the S-2 / S-3 pixel shader work are larger.
